\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\big\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}f\big\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}+\alpha^{4}\big\|D^{2}_{z}f\big\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

We thus complete this proof.
∎

6.2.  Estimates for radial operator

Our goal in this subsection is to study the radial operator and establish some estimates for it.
From now on, we assume that
\xi\in[0,2]
.
We can check that the radial derivative
D_{z}
commutes with the operators
L_{z}^{\alpha}
and
L_{\theta}
.
We thus briefly derive the following two propositions.

Proposition 6.10
.

Assume that
\big|\frac{D_{z}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\big|\leq C_{\beta},
where
C_{\beta}>0
is a constant.
Then there holds

\displaystyle-\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

Proof.

Recalling (
6.3
), we can get easily that

\displaystyle-\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle=\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-\frac{5}{2}\alpha^{3}\langle(D_{z}\phi)^{2},D_{z}^{\ast}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

We thus finish the proof of this proposition.
∎

Proposition 6.11
.

Assume that for any
k=1,2
,

\sum\limits_{l=0}^{k}\big|\frac{D^{l}_{z}D^{k-l}_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\big|\leq C_{\beta}
, where
C_{\beta}>0
is a constant.
Then there exist sufficiently small constants
\alpha>0
and
\varepsilon>0
, such that

\displaystyle-\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1-\xi}{1+\xi}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-\varepsilon\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

(6.21)

Moreover, for any
n\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
, there holds

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

(6.22)

Proof.

We recall (
6.3
), to discover that

\displaystyle-\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle=\langle(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle+\langle 5\alpha D_{z}\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle\triangleq\mathcal{J}_{1}+\mathcal{J}_{2}.

(6.23)

Integration by parts leads to

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\alpha^{2}\langle D_{z}\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}D_{z}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-\alpha^{2}\langle D_{z}\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

(6.24)

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle(D_{z}\phi)^{2},\partial^{2}_{\theta}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle-\alpha^{2}\langle D_{z}\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle(D_{z}\phi)^{2},\partial^{2}_{\theta}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}},

where
\varepsilon>0
is a small constant.
Notice that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}_{\theta}(\mathcal{W}^{2})}{\mathcal{W}^{2}}-4\xi(\xi+1)\cot^{2}(2\theta)=4\xi,\quad\text{for any}~~\xi\in[0,2].

(6.25)

It then follows from (
6.25
), Lemma
6.2
and Lemma
6.4
that

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle(D_{z}\phi)^{2},\partial^{2}_{\theta}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha^{2}\xi(\xi+1)\langle(D_{z}\phi)^{2},\cot^{2}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle+C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\alpha^{2}\xi}{\xi+1}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\left(\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\right)+C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\alpha^{2}\xi}{\xi+1}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

Plugging the estimate above into (
6.24
), we can get that

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{1}\geq\frac{1-\xi}{1+\xi}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

A direct computation yields directly that

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{2}\geq-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

(6.26)

We substitute the estimates of
\mathcal{J}_{1}
and
\mathcal{J}_{2}
into (
6.23
), to obtain (
6.21
) directly.

Again thanks to (
6.3
), we can find that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle=\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi,\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle+5\alpha\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\phi,\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle\triangleq\mathcal{J}_{3}+\mathcal{J}_{4}.

(6.27)

We infer from
\big|\frac{D^{j}_{z,\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\big|\lesssim_{\beta}1
with
j=1,2
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{3}=

\displaystyle\langle(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\partial^{n}_{\theta}\phi\partial^{n+2}_{\theta}\phi,\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\alpha^{2}\langle D_{z}\partial^{n}_{\theta}\phi D_{z}\partial^{n+2}_{\theta}\phi,\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-\alpha^{2}\langle D_{z}\partial^{n}_{\theta}\phi\partial^{n+2}_{\theta}\phi,\sin^{2n}(2\theta)D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle(D_{z}\partial^{n}_{\theta}\phi)^{2},\partial^{2}_{\theta}\big(\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\big)\rangle-\alpha^{2}\langle D_{z}\partial^{n}_{\theta}\phi\partial^{n+2}_{\theta}\phi,\sin^{2n}(2\theta)D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

It is not difficult to deduce that

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{4}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

Inserting the estimates
\mathcal{J}_{3}
and
\mathcal{J}_{4}
into (
6.27
), we obtain (
6.22
) immediately, and thus complete the proof of this proposition.
∎

6.3.  Estimates for angular operator

In this subsection, we aim to investigate the angular operator and derive some estimates for it.
One can easily verify that the radial operator
L_{z}^{\alpha}
commutes with the angular derivative
D_{\theta}
, whereas the angular operator
L_{\theta}
does not.
This occurs because the term
\partial_{\theta}\left(\tan\theta\phi\right)
in
L_{\theta}
does not commute with
D_{\theta}
.
Thus, we establish some estimates for
\partial_{\theta}\left(\tan\theta\phi\right)
in this section.

We define

\widetilde{\phi}\triangleq\frac{\phi}{\cos(\theta)}

and take the notation
D^{-1}_{\theta}f=0
, and thereby derive the following proposition.

Proposition 6.12
.

Assume that for
k=1,2
,
\left|\frac{D^{k}_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\leq C
, where
C>0
is a constant.
Then for
n\in\mathbb{N}
, there holds

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}(\tan\theta\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle\geq\left(\frac{4}{3}-\frac{\xi}{2}\right)\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C\|\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C\sum_{k=0}^{n}\|\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

(6.28)

Proof.

We apply the Leibniz rule, to obtain that

\displaystyle\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}(\tan\theta\phi)=\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}(\sin\theta\widetilde{\phi})=(\sin\theta)\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}+(n+1)(\cos\theta)\partial^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}P_{k}(\theta)\partial^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi},

(6.29)

\displaystyle\partial^{n+2}_{\theta}\phi=\partial^{n+2}_{\theta}((\cos\theta)\widetilde{\phi})=\cos\theta\partial^{n+2}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}-(n+2)(\sin\theta)\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}+\sum_{m=0}^{n}Q_{m}(\theta)\partial^{m}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi},

for some functions
P_{k}(\theta)
,
Q_{m}(\theta)\in C^{\infty}([0,\frac{\pi}{2}])
.
These two identities yield that

\displaystyle~~~-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}(\tan\theta\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

(6.30)

\displaystyle=-\langle\partial_{\theta}^{n+1}(\tan\theta\phi)\partial^{n+2}_{\theta}\phi,\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\langle\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}D_{\theta}\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle+(n+2)\langle(\sin\theta)^{2}(\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~-(n+1)\langle\cos^{2}\theta\partial^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{n+2}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle+\frac{(n+1)(n+2)}{2}\langle\sin(2\theta)\partial^{n}_{\theta}\tilde{\phi}\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\tilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~-\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\langle S_{k}(\theta)\partial^{k}_{\theta}\tilde{\phi}\partial^{n+2}_{\theta}\tilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-\sum_{i=0}^{n+1}\sum_{j=0}^{n}\langle S_{ij}(\theta)\partial^{i}_{\theta}\tilde{\phi}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\tilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{i=1}^{6}\mathcal{K}_{i},

for some functions
S_{k}(\theta)
,
S_{i,j}(\theta)\in C^{\infty}([0,\frac{\pi}{2}])
.
We deal with the terms
\mathcal{K}_{i}

(i=1,\dots,6)
in turn, beginning with
\mathcal{K}_{1}
.
In view of integration by parts, we infer that

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}\langle D_{\theta}(\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle=\frac{1}{4}\langle(\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},D^{\ast}_{\theta}\big(\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\big)\rangle=\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\mathcal{A}_{1}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle,

where
D^{\ast}_{\theta}
is the adjoint operators of
D_{\theta}
, and
\mathcal{A}_{1}\triangleq\left(n+\frac{1}{2}\right)\cos(2\theta)+\frac{1}{2}\frac{D_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}
.
For the term
\mathcal{K}_{2}
, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{2}=\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\mathcal{A}_{2}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle,

where
\mathcal{A}_{2}\triangleq(n+2)\sin^{2}\theta.

As for the term
\mathcal{K}_{3}
, we deduce from
\left|\frac{D^{j}_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\lesssim 1
with
j=1,2
and integration by parts that

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{3}

\displaystyle=\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\mathcal{A}_{3}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-\frac{n+1}{2}\langle(\partial^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\partial^{2}_{\theta}\left[\cos^{2}\theta\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\right]\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\mathcal{A}_{3}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-C\langle(\partial^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\sin^{2(n-1)}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\mathcal{A}_{3}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-C\|\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}},

where
\mathcal{A}_{3}\triangleq(n+1)\cos^{2}\theta.

For the term
\mathcal{K}_{4}
, since
\left|\frac{D_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\lesssim 1
, we see that

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{4}=-\frac{1}{4}(n+1)(n+2)\big\langle\big(\partial^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\big)^{2},D^{\ast}_{\theta}\left[\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\right]\big\rangle\gtrsim-\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

In order to address the term
\mathcal{K}_{5}
, we apply once more
\left|\frac{D_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\lesssim 1
and Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, to find that

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{5}=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\langle S_{k}(\theta)\partial^{k+1}_{\theta}\tilde{\phi}\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\tilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\big\langle\partial^{k}_{\theta}\tilde{\phi}\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\tilde{\phi},D^{\ast}_{\theta}\left[S_{k}(\theta)\sin^{2n-1}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\right]\big\rangle

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\|\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

For the last term
\mathcal{K}_{6}
, again thanks to Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we get that

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{6}\gtrsim-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\|\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

Moreover, it is easy to verify that

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{1}+\mathcal{A}_{2}+\mathcal{A}_{3}=2n\cos^{2}(\theta)+\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{D_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\geq\frac{3-\xi}{2},

(6.31)

where we have used the fact that

\displaystyle-\xi\leq\frac{D_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\leq\xi.

(6.32)

Substituting the estimates for
\mathcal{K}_{i}

(i=1,\cdots,6)
into (
6.30
), and applying (
6.31
), we can obtain (
6.28
) immediately.
∎

Proposition 6.13
.

Assume that for any
k=1,2
,

\sum\limits_{l=0}^{k}\left|\frac{D^{l}_{z}D^{k-l}_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\leq C_{\beta}

where
C_{\beta}>0
is a constant.
Then there exist sufficiently small constants
0<\alpha\leq 1
and
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
such that

\displaystyle-\langle\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle\geq\frac{1-\xi}{2}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-\varepsilon\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}},

(6.33)

and for any
n\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
, there holds

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1-\xi}{2}\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\|D^{j}_{z}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}

(6.34)

\displaystyle-\left(2\varepsilon+C_{\beta}\alpha\right)\sum_{i+j=0}^{1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

Proof.

Firstly, we infer that

\displaystyle\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi)(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi=\frac{1}{2}D_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\widetilde{\phi}+\phi(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi.

This gives that

\displaystyle-\langle\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle D_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\widetilde{\phi},\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-\langle\phi(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi,\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle\triangleq\mathcal{L}_{1}+\mathcal{L}_{2}.

(6.35)

For the first term
\mathcal{L}_{1}
, in view of
\left|\frac{D_{z}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\lesssim_{\beta}1
and integration by parts, we find that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle D_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle D_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{4}\langle\big(D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\big)^{2},D^{\ast}_{\theta}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle-\varepsilon\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|D_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}},

where
\varepsilon>0
is a small constant.
While for the term
\mathcal{L}_{2}
, we infer from
\left|\frac{D^{j}_{z}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\lesssim_{\beta}1
with
j=1,2
and Lemma
6.2
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{2}

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle\phi^{2},(D^{\ast}_{z})^{2}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta}\|\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\|(\cos\theta)D_{z}\tilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta}\|\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

Notice that

\displaystyle(\cos\theta)^{2}\mathcal{W}^{2}-\frac{1}{4}D^{\ast}_{\theta}(\mathcal{W}^{2})=\left[(\cos\theta)^{2}+(\sin\theta)^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\frac{D_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right)\right]\mathcal{W}^{2}\geq\frac{1-\xi}{2}\mathcal{W}^{2},

(6.36)

where we have used (
6.32
).
We continue by substituting the estimates of
\mathcal{L}_{1}
and
\mathcal{L}_{2}
into (
6.35
), and employing (
6.36
), eventually to find that (
6.33
) holds.

With the help of the Leibniz rule, one gets easily that

\displaystyle\partial^{n}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi=(\cos\theta)\partial^{n}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\widetilde{\phi}-(n+1)(\sin\theta)\partial^{n-1}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\widetilde{\phi}+\sum_{m=0}^{n-2}Q_{m}(\theta)\partial^{m}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\widetilde{\phi},

for some
Q_{m}(\theta)\in C^{\infty}([0,\frac{\pi}{2}])
.
This, together with (
6.29
), yields directly that

\displaystyle~~~-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

(6.37)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\langle D_{\theta}\partial^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{n}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-(n+1)\langle\cos^{2}\theta\partial^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{n}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~+(n+1)\langle\sin^{2}\theta\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{n-1}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\langle S_{k}(\theta)\partial^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{n}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~-\sum_{r=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n+r}\sum_{j=0}^{n-r-1}\langle S_{rij}(\theta)\partial^{i}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{j}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{i=1}^{5}\mathcal{M}_{i},

for some
S_{k}(\theta)
,
S_{rij}(\theta)\in C^{\infty}([0,\frac{\pi}{2}])
.
Recalling that
\left|\frac{D_{z}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\lesssim_{\beta}1
, the first term
\mathcal{M}_{1}
is controlled by

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle D_{\theta}\partial^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle\tilde{D}^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{4}\langle(\partial^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},D^{\ast}_{\theta}\big(\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\big)\rangle+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle\tilde{D}^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{1}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha\|\tilde{D}^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

where
\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{1}
is defined by

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{1}=-\left(n+\frac{1}{2}\right)\cos(2\theta)-\frac{1}{2}\frac{D_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}=(n+1)(\sin\theta)^{2}-n(\cos\theta)^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\frac{D_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right).

For the term
\mathcal{M}_{2}
, we deduce from
\left|\frac{D^{j}_{z}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\lesssim_{\beta}1
with
j=1,2
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{2}

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{2}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle+(n+1)\alpha^{2}\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},(\cos\theta)^{2}D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{2}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}(n+1)\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},(\cos\theta)^{2}(D^{\ast}_{z})^{2}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{2}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}},

where
\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{2}=(n+1)(\cos\theta)^{2}.

We now estimate the third term
\mathcal{M}_{3}
, we employ
\left|\frac{D_{z}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|+\left|\frac{D_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\lesssim_{\beta}1
and
\alpha\leq 1
to deduce that

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{3}

\displaystyle=-\alpha^{2}(n+1)\langle\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{n-1}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},(\sin\theta)^{2}\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~-\alpha^{2}(n+1)\langle\tilde{D}^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},(\sin\theta)^{2}D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}(n+1)\langle\partial^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{n-1}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},D^{\ast}_{\theta}((\sin\theta)^{2}\sin^{2n-1}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle~~~-\alpha^{2}(n+1)\langle\tilde{D}^{n+1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},(\sin\theta)^{2}D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle+\alpha^{2}\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{3}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\langle(\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi})^{2},\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{3}\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle-\varepsilon\sum_{i+j=1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}},

where
\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{3}=(n+1)(\sin\theta)^{2}.

We then address the term
\mathcal{M}_{4}
by using
\left|\frac{D_{z}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\lesssim_{\beta}1
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{4}

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\langle S_{k}(\theta)\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{n-k}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle+\alpha^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\langle S_{k}(\theta)\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{n-k}(2\theta)D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\|D^{j}_{z}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

To handle the term
\mathcal{M}_{5}
, we derive from
\left|\frac{D_{z}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\right|\lesssim_{\beta}1
and
\alpha\leq 1
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{5}

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\sum_{r=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n+r}\sum_{j=0}^{n-r-1}\langle S_{rij}(\theta)\partial^{i}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{j}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~+\alpha^{2}\sum_{r=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n+r}\sum_{j=0}^{n-r-1}\langle S_{rij}(\theta)\tilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\tilde{D}^{j}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n-i-j}(2\theta)D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle=-\alpha^{2}\sum_{r=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n+r-1}\sum_{j=0}^{n-r}\langle S_{rij}(\theta)\partial^{i}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{j}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle~~~-\alpha^{2}\sum_{r=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n+r-1}\sum_{j=0}^{n-r-1}\langle S_{rij}(\theta)\partial^{i}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\partial^{j}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},D^{\ast}_{\theta}(\sin^{2n-1}(2\theta)\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle~~~+\alpha^{2}\sum_{r=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n+r}\sum_{j=0}^{n-r-1}\langle S_{rij}(\theta)\tilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\tilde{D}^{j}_{\theta}D_{z}\widetilde{\phi},\sin^{n-i-j}(2\theta)D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2})\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon\sum_{i+j=1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\|D^{j}_{z}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}.

Moreover, it is easy to verify that

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{1}+\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{2}+\tilde{\mathcal{A}}_{3}=1+(2n+1)\sin^{2}\theta-\frac{1}{2}\bigg(1+\frac{D_{\theta}\mathcal{W}}{\mathcal{W}}\bigg)\geq\frac{1-\xi}{2}\mathcal{W}^{2}.

(6.38)

Substituting the estimates of
\mathcal{M}_{i}

(i=1,\cdots,5)
into (
6.37
), then using the fact that
0<\alpha\leq 1
and
0<\varepsilon\leq 1
, finally using (
6.38
), we get (
6.34
) directly.
∎

6.4.  The elliptic estimates with purely radial weight

In this subsection, we intend to establish the weighted elliptic estimates with purely radial weight.

Lemma 6.14
.

Assume that
\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}=0
and that for
j=1,2
,
\big|\frac{D^{j}_{z}\mathcal{W}_{z}}{\mathcal{W}_{z}}\big|\leq C_{\beta}
,
where
C_{\beta}>0
is a constant. Then for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
, there exists a sufficiently small constant
\alpha>0
such that

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\|D^{n+1}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\leq C_{\beta}\|D^{n}_{z}f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

Proof.

Recalling the definition of
L_{\theta}
in (
6.4
), it is easy to verify that its adjoint operator
L^{*}_{\theta}
satisfies

L^{*}_{\theta}(K(\theta))=0.

For any
\alpha>0
, there holds

\displaystyle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\langle D^{n}_{z}\phi,K\rangle_{\theta})=\langle D^{n}_{z}(L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi)+L_{\theta}(\phi)),K\rangle_{\theta}=D^{n}_{z}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}=0.

This, combining a similar derivation of (
4.6
), leads us to get that

\displaystyle\langle D^{n}_{z}\phi,K\rangle_{\theta}=0.

(6.39)

According to the completeness of the Fourier series, we obtain the odd extension of
D_{z}^{n}\phi

(n\geq 0)
on
[0,\pi]
as:

\displaystyle D^{n}_{z}\phi(z,\theta)=\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\phi^{n}_{k}(z)\sin(2k\theta),~~~\phi_{k}^{n}(z)\triangleq\int_{0}^{\pi}D^{n}_{z}\phi(z,\theta)\sin(2k\theta)d\theta.

(6.40)

With this odd extension at hand, the unique identity is derived from (
6.39
) and Lemma
6.1
that

\displaystyle\phi^{n}_{1}=\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}(-1)^{k}\frac{15k}{(4k^{2}-1)(4k^{2}-9)}\phi^{n}_{k}.

(6.41)

It is easy to verify that
15k^{2}\leq(4k^{2}-1)(4k^{2}-9)
holds for any
k\geq 2
.
This implies that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{225k^{2}}{(4k^{2}-1)^{2}(4k^{2}-9)^{2}}\leq\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{1}{k^{2}}\leq 1.

(6.42)

We employ (
6.41
), (
6.42
) and Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, to get that

\displaystyle\|\phi^{n}_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\leq\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{225k^{2}}{(4k^{2}-1)^{2}(4k^{2}-9)^{2}}\|\phi^{n}_{k}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\leq\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\|\phi^{n}_{k}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.43)

On the other hand, the equation (
6
) gives that

\displaystyle\langle D^{n}_{z}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),D^{n}_{z}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle+\langle D^{n}_{z}L_{\theta}(\phi),D^{n}_{z}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle=\langle f,D^{n}_{z}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle.

(6.44)

Recalling (
6.3
) and (
6.4
), then applying integration by parts, we achieve that

\displaystyle\langle D^{n}_{z}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),D^{n}_{z}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\|D^{n+1}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\langle(D^{n}_{z}\phi)^{2},(D^{\ast}_{z})^{2}(\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z})\rangle+\frac{5\alpha}{2}\langle(D^{n}_{z}\phi)^{2},D^{\ast}_{z}(\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z})\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D^{n+1}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha\|D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}},

and

\displaystyle\langle D^{n}_{z}L_{\theta}(\phi),D^{n}_{z}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle=

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-6\|D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\frac{1}{2}\|(\sec\theta)D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

We combine these two estimates above and (
6.44
) together, then take
\alpha
and
\varepsilon
small enough, to discover that

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\|D^{n+1}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-7\|D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\frac{1}{2}\|(\sec\theta)D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|D_{z}^{n}f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.45)

We then deduce from (
6.40
), the orthogonality of
\left\{\sin(2k\theta)\right\}_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}
and
\left\{\cos(2k\theta)\right\}_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}
that

\displaystyle\|D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}=\mathop{\sum}_{k=1}^{\infty}\|\phi^{n}_{k}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}},\quad\|\partial_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}=\mathop{\sum}_{k=1}^{\infty}4k^{2}\|\phi^{n}_{k}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.46)

In view of (
6.43
) and (
6.46
), we have

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\leq 2\mathop{\sum}_{k=2}^{\infty}4k^{2}\|\phi^{n}_{k}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

Combining this with (
6.43
), (
6.45
) and (
6.46
), we get that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-7\|D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle=-3\|\phi^{n}_{1}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\mathop{\sum}_{k=2}^{\infty}(4k^{2}-7)\|\phi^{n}_{k}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\geq\mathop{\sum}_{k=2}^{\infty}(4k^{2}-10)\|\phi^{n}_{k}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

(6.47)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{8}\mathop{\sum}_{k=2}^{\infty}4k^{2}\|\phi^{n}_{k}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\geq\frac{3}{16}\|\partial_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

It follows that

\displaystyle 7\|D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\leq\frac{13}{16}\|\partial_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.48)

Substituting (
6.47
) and (
6.48
) into (
6.45
), we find that

\displaystyle\|D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D^{n+1}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|(\sec\theta)D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|D_{z}^{n}f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

This completes the proof of Lemma
6.14
.
∎

For any
n\in\mathbb{N}
, we introduce
\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}([0,\infty)\times[0,\frac{\pi}{2}])
equipped with the Sobolev norm containing exclusively radial weight as:

\displaystyle\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\triangleq\sum_{i+j=0}^{n}\|D^{i}_{z}D^{j}_{\theta}f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

Proposition 6.15
.

Assume that
\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}=0
and that for
j=1,2
,
\left|\frac{D^{j}_{z}\mathcal{W}_{z}}{\mathcal{W}_{z}}\right|\leq C_{\beta}
where
C_{\beta}>0
is a constant. Then for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
, there exists a sufficiently small constant
\alpha>0
such that

\displaystyle\alpha^{4}\|D^{2}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\leq C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.49)

Proof.

We will prove this proposition by induction on
n
.

Step 1: For the base case
n=0
.

Along a similar way as (
6.24
) with
\partial^{2}_{\theta}(\mathcal{W}_{z}^{2})=0
and (
6.26
), we can deduce that

\displaystyle-\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle\geq\alpha^{2}\|\partial_{\theta}D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-\varepsilon\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.50)

Recalling the definition of
L_{\theta}
in (
6.4
), and taking
n=0
and
\xi=0
in Proposition
6.12
, we then have

\displaystyle-\langle L_{\theta}(\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

\displaystyle=\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-\langle\partial_{\theta}(\tan\theta\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle+6\langle\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

(6.51)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\frac{4}{3}\|\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\|\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

Then, we recall the equation (
6
), to discover that

\displaystyle\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle+\langle L_{\theta}\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle=\langle f,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle.

(6.52)

Plugging (
6.50
) and (
6.51
) into (
6.52
), choosing
\varepsilon
suitably small, and applying Lemma
6.2
, one gets that

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\|\partial_{\theta}D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

(6.53)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

The same argument as Proposition
6.10
, we can deduce that

\displaystyle-\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.54)

We then deduce from Proposition
6.13
with
\xi=0
and
\varepsilon=\frac{1}{4}
that

\displaystyle-\langle\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{2}}{4}\|D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\|\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}},

which, together with Lemma
6.2
, implies that

\displaystyle-\langle L_{\theta}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle-\langle\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle+6\langle\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

(6.55)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\alpha^{2}}{4}\|D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-\frac{\alpha^{4}}{2}\|D^{2}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\|\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

Combining the equation (
6
), (
6.54
) and (
6.55
) together, we find that

\displaystyle\alpha^{4}\|D^{2}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.56)

By virtue of (
6.53
), (
6.56
) and Lemma
6.14
, we can deduce that

\displaystyle\alpha^{4}\|D^{2}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|\partial_{\theta}D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}},

(6.57)

which implies that (
6.49
) holds true for
n=0
.

Step 2: For the case
n\geq 1
.
We continue our proof by induction argument on
n\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
. First of all, assume that (
6.49
) holds for
n-1
, namely

\displaystyle\alpha^{4}\|D^{2}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.58)

With the help of Lemmas
6.7
-
6.9
and the induction assumption (
6.58
), we find that

\displaystyle~~~~~~\alpha^{4}\|D^{2}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\sum_{i+j=n}\left(\alpha^{4}\|D^{i+2}_{z}D_{\theta}^{j}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}^{i+1}D_{\theta}^{j}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|D_{z}^{i}D_{\theta}^{j}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\sum_{k=0}^{n}\left(\alpha^{4}\left(\|D^{k+2}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|D^{k}_{\theta}D_{z}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\right)+\alpha^{2}\left(\|D^{k+1}_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|D^{k}_{\theta}D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~\left.+\left(\|D_{z}^{k}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|D_{\theta}^{k}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{4}\left(\|D^{n+2}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|D^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\right)+\alpha^{2}\left(\|D^{n+1}_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|D^{n}_{\theta}D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\right)

\displaystyle~~~~~+\left(\|D_{z}^{n}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|D_{\theta}^{n}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\sum_{i+j=2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{4}\|D^{n+2}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

Hence, we only need to prove that

\displaystyle\sum_{i+j=2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{4}\|D^{n+2}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.59)

We divide into the following two steps to prove (
6.59
).

Step 2.1.

Our targe in to prove that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|D^{n}_{z}f\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.60)

Notice that the operators
D_{z}
and
L^{\alpha}_{z}+L_{\theta}
are commutative, (
6.60
) can be deduced by a similar way as (
6.57
).

Step 2.2.
Our goal is to show that

\displaystyle\sum_{i+j=2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.61)

We can deduce from Lemma
6.7
, Proposition
6.11
and the induction assumption (
6.58
) that for
n\geq 1
,

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.62)

On the other hand, some direct computations give that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L_{\theta}(\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle=\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}(\tan(\theta)\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle+6\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi,\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle.

(6.63)

We infer from Proposition
6.12
with
\xi=0
that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C\|\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}}}-C\sum_{k=0}^{n}\|\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.64)

For any
1\leq m\leq n
, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\|\tilde{D}^{m}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{m}\|\sin^{m}(2\theta)(\cos\theta)^{j-m-1}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{m}\|\sin^{j-1}(2\theta)\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.65)

We then employ the induction assumption (
6.58
), Lemma
6.2
and Lemma
6.7
, to find that

\displaystyle\|\frac{\partial_{\theta}^{j}\phi}{\sin^{1-j}(2\theta)}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}},\quad\text{for}~~j=0,1,

\displaystyle\|\sin^{j-1}(2\theta)\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\|\tilde{D}^{j-2}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\|D^{j-2}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{j-2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}},\quad\text{for any}~~2\leq j\leq m.

Substituting these three estimates above into (
6.65
), then employing the induction assumption (
6.58
) and Lemma
6.7
, one obtains that for any
1\leq m\leq n
,

\displaystyle\|\tilde{D}^{m}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{m-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.66)

For any
1\leq m\leq n
, by some direct calculations, we have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{D}_{\theta}^{m-1}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{m}\|\sin^{m-1}(2\theta)(\cos\theta)^{j-m-1}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{m}\|\sin^{j-2}(2\theta)\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.67)

Again thanks to the induction assumption (
6.58
), Lemma
6.2
and Lemma
6.7
, we get that

\displaystyle\|\frac{\partial_{\theta}^{j}\phi}{(\sin(2\theta))^{2-j}}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}},\quad\text{for}~~j=0,1,

\displaystyle\|\sin^{j-2}(2\theta)\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\|\tilde{D}^{j-2}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\|D^{j-2}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{j-2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}},\quad\text{for any}~~2\leq j\leq m.

We then insert these three estimates into (
6.67
), to discover that for any
1\leq m\leq n
,

\displaystyle\|\tilde{D}_{\theta}^{m-1}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{m-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.68)

Substituting (
6.66
) and (
6.68
) into (
6.64
), we find that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.69)

With the help of integration by parts, we can deduce that

\displaystyle\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi,\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle=

\displaystyle-\langle\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\left(\partial^{n+1}_{\theta}\phi\right)^{2},\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\langle\partial_{\theta}^{2}\left(\sin^{2n}(2\theta)\right)\left(\partial^{n}_{\theta}\phi\right)^{2},\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

(6.70)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\widetilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

Again applying Lemma
6.2
, Lemma
6.7
and the induction assumption (
6.58
), and following a derivation analogous to (
6.66
) and (
6.68
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi,\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.71)

The combination of (
6.63
), (
6.69
) and (
6.71
), yields directly that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L_{\theta}(\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle\geq\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.72)

Recalling the equation (
6
), applying (
6.62
) and (
6.72
), and taking
\varepsilon
suitably small, we infer that

\displaystyle\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.73)

Along a similar way as Proposition
6.10
, it then follows from Lemma
6.7
and the induction assumption (
6.58
) that for
n\geq 1
,

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

(6.74)

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

While for
L_{\theta}
, we have

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L_{\theta}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

(6.75)

\displaystyle~~~+6\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

\displaystyle\triangleq\mathcal{N}_{1}+\mathcal{N}_{2}+\mathcal{N}_{3}.

We now control three terms in the right-hand side of (
6.75
) term by term.
The application of (
6.73
) yields directly that

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{1}\geq-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\geq-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.76)

For the second term, employing Proposition
6.13
with
\xi=0
, we get that for
n\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{2}\geq-(2\varepsilon+C_{\beta}\alpha)\sum^{1}_{i+j=0}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\|D_{z}^{j}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.77)

We then estimate the terms in the right-hand side of (
6.77
) in turn.
Firstly, it follows from Lemma
6.2
that

\displaystyle\sum_{i+j=1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i+j=1}\sum_{k=0}^{n+j}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\sin^{n}(2\theta)(\cos\theta)^{k-n-j-1}\partial^{k}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

(6.78)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i+j=1}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}(\cos\theta)^{-j-1}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|(\cos\theta)^{-1}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i+j=1}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\partial_{\theta}^{j+1}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i+j=1}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\sum_{l=0}^{j+1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}(\sin(2\theta))^{k+l-j-1}\partial_{\theta}^{k+l}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

For any
0\leq l\leq j+1-k
, it follows from Lemma
6.2
that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}(\sin(2\theta))^{k+l-j-1}\partial_{\theta}^{k+l}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\partial_{\theta}^{j+1}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

For any
j+2-k\leq l\leq j+1
, one can deduce directly that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}(\sin(2\theta))^{k+l-j-1}\partial_{\theta}^{k+l}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}_{\theta}^{k+l-j-1}\partial_{\theta}^{j+1}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim\sum_{m=0}^{k}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}_{\theta}^{m}\partial_{\theta}^{j+1}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

We plug these two estimates into (
6.78
), then employ Lemma
6.7
, the induction assumption (
6.58
) and (
6.73
), to discover that

\displaystyle\sum_{i+j=1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i+j=1}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{j+1}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.79)

After some direct calculations, one gets, by Lemma
6.2
, that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\sum_{i=0}^{k}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\sin^{k}(2\theta)(\cos\theta)^{i-k-1}\partial^{i}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

(6.80)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\|(\cos\theta)^{-1}(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\widetilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\|\partial_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\tilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

For
j=0
, taking advantage of Lemma
6.2
, Lemma
6.7
and the induction assumption (
6.58
), we can deduce from a similar way as (
6.66
) and (
6.68
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\|\partial_{\theta}\tilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.81)

For
j=1
, again thanks to Lemma
6.2
, Lemma
6.7
and the induction assumption (
6.58
), it is enough to prove that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\|\alpha D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\tilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\alpha D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\left(\|\alpha D_{z}\tilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}+\|\alpha D_{z}\tilde{D}^{i-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\right)

(6.82)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\|\alpha D_{z}\tilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

Substituting (
6.81
) and (
6.82
) into (
6.80
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.83)

Inserting (
6.66
), (
6.79
) and (
6.83
) into (
6.77
), one obtains that

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{2}\gtrsim_{\beta}-\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.84)

As for the last term, we can deduce from the induction assumption (
6.58
) and Lemma
6.2
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{3}

\displaystyle\geq-\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\geq-\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\|\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\right)

(6.85)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n-1}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

We substitute (
6.76
), (
6.84
) and (
6.85
) into (
6.75
), to discover that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L_{\theta}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\mathcal{W}^{2}_{z}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.86)

Combining the equation (
6
), (
6.74
) and (
6.86
), and taking
\varepsilon
suitably small, we infer that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.87)

In view of (
6.73
) and (
6.87
), we conclude that (
6.61
) holds true.

The combination of (
6.60
) in
Step 2.1
and (
6.61
) in
Step 2.2
yields (
6.59
) immediately.
We thus complete the proof of Proposition
6.15
by standard induction argument.
∎

6.5.  The elliptic estimates with mixed weight

In the previous section, we obtained the elliptic estimates with purely radial weights.
Building upon this foundation, we now focus on the mixed weight and attempt to establish the elliptic estimate with mixed weight.
Firstly, for any parameters
\alpha
,
\beta
,
\eta
and
\lambda
, which are defined in (
1.12
), we denote that

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}\triangleq\sum_{i=0}^{n}\|D^{i}_{z}f\mathcal{W}^{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{0\leq i+j\leq n,j\geq 1}\|D^{i}_{z}\tilde{D}^{j}_{\theta}f\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

where
\mathcal{W}^{\eta}\triangleq\mathcal{W}_{z}\sin^{-\frac{\eta}{2}}(2\theta)
and
\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}\triangleq\mathcal{W}_{z}\sin^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(2\theta)
.
Then, we derive the following elliptic estimates.

Proposition 6.16
.

Assume that
\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}=0
and that
j=1,2
,
\left|\frac{D^{j}_{z}\mathcal{W}_{z}}{\mathcal{W}_{z}}\right|\leq C_{\beta}
, where
C_{\beta}>0
is a constant.
Then for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
, there exists a sufficiently small constant
\alpha>0
such that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}+\|(\alpha D_{z})\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}\leq C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

(6.88)

Proof.

We will prove (
6.88
) by induction on
n
.

Step 1: For the base case
n=0
.

Since
0<\eta<1
, we deduce from Lemma
6.2
and Proposition
6.11
and Proposition
6.15
that

\displaystyle-\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1-\eta}{1+\eta}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-\varepsilon\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

(6.89)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1-\eta}{1+\eta}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-\varepsilon\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

While for
L_{\theta}
, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle-\langle L_{\theta}(\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle=

\displaystyle\langle\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle-\langle\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle+6\langle\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle.

(6.90)

Again utilizing the condition
0<\eta<1
, we apply Proposition
6.12
and Proposition
6.15
with
n=0
and
\xi=\eta
, combined with Lemma
6.2
, to obtain that

\displaystyle-\langle\partial_{\theta}(\tan(\theta)\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C\|\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

(6.91)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

It then follows from
0<\eta<1
, Lemma
6.2
and Proposition
6.15
that

\displaystyle 6\langle\phi,\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\|\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}\geq-\varepsilon\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\|\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

(6.92)

\displaystyle\leq-\varepsilon\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

Substituting (
6.91
) and (
6.92
) into (
6.90
), and choosing
\varepsilon
suitably small, one obtains that

\displaystyle-\langle L_{\theta}(\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.93)

Recalling (
6
), then employing (
6.89
) and (
6.93
), finally choosing
\varepsilon
suitably small, we infer that

\displaystyle\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}.

(6.94)

Owing to
0<\eta<1
, by virtue of Lemma
6.2
and Proposition
6.10
, Proposition
6.15
with
n=0
, we get that

\displaystyle-\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}

(6.95)

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

On the other hand, it is not difficult to find that

\displaystyle-\langle L_{\theta}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle-\langle\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle

(6.96)

\displaystyle~~~+6\langle\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle.

We can derive that

\displaystyle\langle\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle\geq-\varepsilon\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}.

(6.97)

Then, we deduce from Lemma
6.2
, Lemma
6.9
, Proposition
6.13
and Proposition
6.15
with
n=0
that

\displaystyle-\langle\partial_{\theta}(\tan(\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}

(6.98)

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon\alpha^{2}\|\partial_{\theta}D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon\big(\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}\big)-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

Using a similar derivation of (
6.92
), we obtain that

\displaystyle 6\langle\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle\leq

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.99)

Inserting (
6.97
)-(
6.99
) into (
6.96
), one gets that

\displaystyle-\langle L_{\theta}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\eta})^{2}\rangle\geq-\varepsilon\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}_{z}}}.

(6.100)

Applying (
6
), (
6.95
) and (
6.100
), and choosing
\varepsilon
suitably small, we deduce that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}.

(6.101)

Therefore, we conclude from (
6.94
) and (
6.101
) that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}.

(6.102)

This completes the base case
(n=0)
of the induction, with (
6.88
) following directly from Lemma
6.9
.

Step 2: For the case
n\geq 1
.

We now proceed with the induction argument on the parameter
n\in\mathbb{N}
.
As our induction hypothesis, we assume the validity of estimate (
6.88
) for the case
n-1
, which explicitly takes the form:

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}.

(6.103)

We intend to show that (
6.88
) holds for
n
.
Actually, we can deduce from Lemma
6.7
, Lemma
6.9
and (
6.103
) that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}+\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}+\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\|D^{n}_{z}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\sum_{i+j=n,j\geq 1}\left(\|D^{i}_{z}\tilde{D}^{j}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+\|D^{i}_{z}\tilde{D}^{j}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\right).

Hence, we only need to show that for any
n\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i+j=n,j\geq 1}\left(\|D^{i}_{z}\tilde{D}^{j}_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+\|D^{i}_{z}\tilde{D}^{j}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\right)

(6.104)

\displaystyle\quad+\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\|D^{n}_{z}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.~

We divide the proof into the following three steps.

Step 2.1.

In this step, we aim to prove that for any
n\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\|\partial_{\theta}D^{n}_{z}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|D^{n}_{z}f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

(6.105)

Since the operators
D_{z}
and
L^{\alpha}_{z}+L_{\theta}
are commutative, we can use a similar derivation of (
6.102
), to deduce that (
6.105
) holds.

Step 2.2.

Our goal in this step is to prove that for any
n\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i+j=2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

(6.106)

For the case
n=1
, we reapplying Proposition
6.11
in combination with Lemma
6.2
and (
6.105
), we establish that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\tilde{D}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

(6.107)

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{1}}.

One deduces from Proposition
6.11
and the induction assumption (
6.103
) that for any
n\geq 2
,

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

(6.108)

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}.

Combining (
6.107
) and (
6.108
) together, we find that for any
n\geq 1
,

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\geq\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

(6.109)

For the angular operator
L_{\theta}
, we recall (
6.4
), to get that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L_{\theta}(\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi,\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}(\tan\theta\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

(6.110)

\displaystyle~~~+6\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi,\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle.

Applying Proposition
6.12
with
n\geq 1
and
\xi=\lambda
, we infer that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}(\tan\theta\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C\|\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C\sum_{k=0}^{n}\|\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}.

(6.111)

In view of Lemma
6.2
and the induction assumption (
6.103
), we obtain that for any
1\leq m\leq n
,

\displaystyle\|\tilde{D}^{m}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{m}\|\sin^{m}(2\theta)(\cos\theta)^{j-m-1}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

(6.112)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{m}\|\sin^{j-1}(2\theta)\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\frac{\phi}{\sin(2\theta)}\big\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+\big\|\partial_{\theta}\phi\big\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\|\sin^{j}(2\theta)\partial^{j+1}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\sum_{j=1}^{m-1}\|\tilde{D}^{j}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{m-1}}.

Similar argument leads us to get that

\displaystyle\|\tilde{D}^{m-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}\|\sin^{m-1}(2\theta)(\cos\theta)^{j-m}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}\|\sin^{j-1}(2\theta)\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{m-1}}.

We then substitute two estimates above into (
6.111
), to find that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}(\tan\theta\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}.

(6.113)

It is easy to verify that

\displaystyle 6\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi,\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\geq

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

(6.114)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\|\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\sum_{k=1}^{n-1}\|\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}.

Plugging (
6.113
) and (
6.114
) into (
6.110
), and choosing
\varepsilon
suitably small, we find that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L_{\theta}(\phi),\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+\frac{1}{2}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}.

(6.115)

Recalling the equation (
6
), then employing (
6.109
) and (
6.115
), finally taking
\varepsilon
suitably small, we infer that

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

(6.116)

It follows from a similar way as Proposition
6.10
that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

(6.117)

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|D_{z}\tilde{D}^{n-1}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}.

As for
n=1
, applying (
6.105
) and (
6.117
), we can get that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{3}\|D_{z}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{1}}.

Using the induction assumption (
6.103
), we can deduce from (
6.117
) that for any
n\geq 2
