\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}.

A combination of two estimates above yields that

\displaystyle-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

(6.118)

While for
L_{\theta}
defined in (
6.4
), one deduces that

\displaystyle\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L_{\theta}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

(6.119)

\displaystyle~~~+\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}((\tan\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~-6\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle.

With the help of (
6.116
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\left|\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+C_{\varepsilon}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

(6.120)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

Using Proposition
6.13
with
\xi=\lambda
, we find that

\displaystyle\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}(\tan(\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\sum^{1}_{i+j=0}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}.

(6.121)

Thanks to (
6.116
), the induction assumption (
6.103
) and Lemma
6.2
, we see that

\displaystyle\sum_{i+j=1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i+j=1}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{j+1}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

(6.122)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i+j=1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{j+1}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+\sum_{i+j=1}\sum_{k=1}^{n-1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{j+1}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

It then follows from Lemma
6.2
, the induction assumption (
6.103
) and (
6.105
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\widetilde{\phi}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\sin^{i-1}(2\theta)\partial^{i}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

(6.123)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\|\partial_{\theta}(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\tilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\tilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=0}^{1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}+\sum_{i=1}^{n-1}\sum_{k+j=2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{j}\tilde{D}^{i}_{\theta}\partial^{k}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

Substituting (
6.112
) with
m=n
, (
6.122
) and (
6.123
) into (
6.121
), we find that

\displaystyle\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}(\tan(\theta)\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

(6.124)

It is easy to verify that

\displaystyle\left|6\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi,(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+C_{\varepsilon}\|\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}

(6.125)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+C_{\varepsilon}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\|\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n-1}}.

We insert (
6.120
), (
6.124
) and (
6.125
) into (
6.119
), to discover that

\displaystyle\left|\langle\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}L_{\theta}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi(\mathcal{W}^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\right|\leq\varepsilon\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

(6.126)

Combining (
6.118
) and (
6.126
) together, and taking
\varepsilon
suitably small, we infer that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{n}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

(6.127)

Therefore, we conclude from (
6.116
) and (
6.127
) that (
6.106
) holds.

Step 2.3.
In this step, we intend to show that

\displaystyle\sum_{i+j=n,i,1\leq j\leq n-1}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\tilde{D}^{j}_{\theta}(D^{i}_{z}\phi)\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\tilde{D}^{j}_{\theta}(D^{i}_{z}\phi)\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

(6.128)

We have shown in (
6.106
) that for any
1\leq k\leq n
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i+j=2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{k}}.

Along a similar way as (
6.105
), we can deduce from the estimate above that

\displaystyle\sum_{i+j=2}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{i}\tilde{D}^{k}_{\theta}\partial^{j}_{\theta}D_{z}^{n-k}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\mathcal{W}^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|D^{n-k}_{z}f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{k}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\tilde{\mathcal{H}}^{n}}.

This implies that (
6.128
) holds.

Combining (
6.105
) in
Step 2.1
, (
6.106
) in
Step 2.2
and (
6.128
) in
Step 2.3
, we can obtain (
6.104
) immediately.
As a consequence, we complete the proof of this proposition by the standard induction argument.
∎

Recalling Lemma
6.7
and Proposition
6.16
, and taking
\mathcal{W}_{z}=w_{z}
or
w_{z}^{\ast}
, we can derive the following two corollaries.

Corollary 6.17
.

Assume that
\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}=0
and
f\in\mathcal{H}^{n}
, there exists a sufficiently small constant
\alpha>0
such that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}}+\|(\alpha D_{z})\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n}}.

Corollary 6.18
.

Assume that
\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}=0
and
f\in\mathcal{H}^{n,\ast}
, there exists a sufficiently small constant
\alpha>0
such that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n,\ast}}+\|(\alpha D_{z})\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n,\ast}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{n,\ast}}.

Now, it remains to estimate
\mathcal{E}^{n}
-norm.
More precisely, we obtain the following proposition.

Proposition 6.19
.

Assume that
\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}=0
.
Then for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
, there exists a sufficiently small constant
\alpha>0
such that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}D_{z}^{n}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}+\|(\alpha D_{z})\partial_{\theta}D_{z}^{n}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}D_{z}^{n}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|D_{z}^{n}f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}.

Proof.

It follows from Proposition
6.15
that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w_{z}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w_{z}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w_{z}}}.

(6.129)

Owing to
|\lambda-1|\leq\frac{\alpha}{\beta}
, in view of (
6.129
), Lemma
6.2
, Lemma
6.9
, Proposition
6.11
with
\xi=\lambda
and Proposition
6.16
with
n=0
, one obtains that

\displaystyle-\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\alpha}{\beta}\|(\alpha D_{z})\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C\varepsilon\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|\partial_{\theta}D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w_{z}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\alpha^{2}\|\partial_{\theta}D_{z}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{\alpha}{\beta}\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C\left(\frac{\alpha}{\beta}+\varepsilon\right)\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\eta}}}.

By virtue of Lemma
6.2
, Proposition
6.12
, Proposition
6.16
, we infer that

\displaystyle-\langle L_{\theta}(\phi),\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C\|\partial_{\theta}^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\eta}}}-\varepsilon\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C_{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\eta}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\eta}}}.

These two estimates above, together with the equation (
6
), yields directly by taking
\varepsilon
and
\alpha
suitably small that

\displaystyle\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}+\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}.

(6.130)

Applying Lemma
6.2
, Proposition
6.10
and Proposition
6.16
, we find that

\displaystyle-\langle L^{\alpha}_{z}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|(\alpha D_{z})\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\eta}}}\geq\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\eta}}}.

Since
|\lambda-1|\leq\frac{\alpha}{\beta}
, we then deduce from Proposition
6.13
with
\xi=\lambda
and Lemma
6.2
that

\displaystyle-\langle L_{\theta}(\phi),(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\varepsilon\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}-\left(\frac{\alpha}{\beta}+\varepsilon\right)\|(\alpha D_{z})\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}

\displaystyle\geq-\left(\frac{\alpha}{\beta}+\varepsilon\right)\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}-C_{\beta,\varepsilon}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}.

Collecting the two estimates above and the equation (
6
), and taking
\varepsilon
suitably small, we find that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}+\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}.

(6.131)

Combining Lemma
6.9
with estimates (
6.130
) and (
6.131
), and selecting
\alpha
sufficiently small, we obtain that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}+\|(\alpha D_{z})\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}.

Since
D_{z}
commutes with operators
L_{z}^{\alpha}
and
L_{\theta}
, we can easily derive from the estimate above that for any
n\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}D_{z}^{n}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}+\|(\alpha D_{z})\partial_{\theta}D_{z}^{n}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}D_{z}^{n}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|D_{z}^{n}f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{L}^{2}_{w^{\lambda}}}.

We thus complete the proof of Proposition
6.19
.
∎

Based on Proposition
6.19
, we obtain the following corollary directly by recalling the definition of
\mathcal{E}^{n}
.

Corollary 6.20
.

Assume that
\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}=0
and
f\in\mathcal{E}^{n}
.
Then there exists a sufficiently small constant
\alpha>0
such that

\displaystyle\|(\alpha D_{z})^{2}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{n}}+\|(\alpha D_{z})\partial_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{n}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\phi\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{n}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{n}}.

7
Estimates of transport term

In the previous section, we have established the elliptic estimates for equation (
6
), namely Corollaries
6.17
,
6.18
and
6.20
.
Building upon these corollaries, we now begin to study the transport term in this section:

T=\frac{U(\Phi)}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F+V(\Phi)\alpha D_{z}F.

We stress that estimating this transport term is crucial for the analysis of the critical regularity problem.
We need to employ some special structures, such as the null structure.
Recall from (
4.13
) that the transport term
T
admits the following decomposition as:

T=T_{g}+T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}},

where for any function
f
(representing either
g
or
F^{\ast}_{\gamma}
), the operator
T_{f}
is defined as

\displaystyle T_{f}\triangleq\frac{U(\Phi)}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}f+V(\Phi)\alpha D_{z}f.

We recall that the Sobolev spaces
\mathcal{H}^{-1}
,
\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}
and
\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}
are respectively defined by (
5.5
)-(
5.7
), whereas the definitions of
\mathcal{H}^{2}
,
\mathcal{H}^{*,2}
and
\mathcal{E}^{2}
are provided in Section
2
.

7.1.  Basic lemmas and the null structure

Let
D=[0,\infty)\times[0,\frac{\pi}{2}]
and
\beta\in(0,1]
.
Before proceeding, we first present some useful lemmas in this section, which are particularly crucial when dealing with the transport term later.

Lemma 7.1
.

Let
f(z,\theta)|_{\partial D}=0
. Then we have

\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}}\leq C\|D_{z}fw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}},~~~~\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}}\leq C\|D_{z}fw^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}},

and

\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|D_{\theta}fw_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}.

Proof.

Since
\beta\in(0,1]
, one gets, by recalling the definitions of
D_{z}
and
w_{z}
in Section
2
, that

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}|\partial_{z}f|dz\right)^{2}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\infty}|D_{z}f|^{2}(w_{z})^{2}dz\int_{0}^{\infty}(zw_{z})^{-2}dz

(7.1)

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D_{z}fw_{z}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{z}}\int_{0}^{\infty}(1+z)^{-2}dz\lesssim\|D_{z}fw_{z}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{z}}.

From the definition of
w^{\ast}_{z}
in Section
2
, the same manner yields immediately that

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{z}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D_{z}fw^{\ast}_{z}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{z}}\int_{0}^{\infty}(1+z)^{-\frac{3}{2}}dz\lesssim\|D_{z}fw^{\ast}_{z}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{z}}.

(7.2)

Following the definition (
1.12
), the parameter
\lambda
satisfies
\lambda=1+\frac{\alpha}{10\beta}>1
.
It then follows from a similar way that

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}|D_{\theta}f|^{2}(w^{\lambda}_{\theta})^{2}d\theta\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}(\sin(2\theta)w^{\lambda}_{\theta})^{-2}d\theta\lesssim\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\|D_{\theta}fw^{\lambda}_{\theta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\theta}}.

(7.3)

We thus complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

Remembering the definitions of some weight functions
w_{z}
,
w_{\theta}^{\eta}
,
w^{\eta}
,
w^{\ast,\eta}
,
w^{\lambda}
and
w^{\ast,\lambda}
in Section
2
, we have the following corollary.
Its proof is similar to that of Lemma
7.1
, so it is omitted here.

Corollary 7.2
.

Let
f(z,\theta)|_{\partial D}=0
. Then we have

\|fw_{\theta}^{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{\theta}}\leq C\|D_{z}fw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}},~~~~\|fw_{\theta}^{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{\theta}}\leq C\|D_{z}fw^{\ast,\eta}\|_{L^{2}},

and

\|fw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|D_{\theta}fw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}.

Moreover, there holds

\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|D_{\theta}D_{z}fw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}},~~~\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|D_{\theta}D_{z}fw^{\ast,\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}.

Lemma 7.3
.

Let
f(z,\theta)|_{\partial D}=0
and
\tilde{G}_{f}(z)=\frac{3}{4\alpha}z^{-\frac{5}{\alpha}}\int_{0}^{z}\rho^{\frac{5}{\alpha}-1}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}d\rho
. Then we have

\|D^{k}_{z}\tilde{G}_{f}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{k}},~~~~\|D^{k}_{z}\tilde{G}_{f}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{k,\ast}},

and

\alpha\|D^{k+1}_{z}\tilde{G}_{f}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{k}},~~~~\alpha\|D^{k+1}_{z}\tilde{G}_{f}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{k,\ast}}.

Proof.

After some direct calculations, it is not difficult to check that
w_{z}\approx 1+z^{-\frac{2}{\beta}}
.
This, together with integration by parts, yields that

\displaystyle\|\tilde{G}_{f}w_{z}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{z}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{G}_{f}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{z}}+\|\tilde{G}_{f}z^{-\frac{2}{\beta}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{z}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\frac{\alpha}{|\alpha-10|}\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{z}(z^{-\frac{10}{\alpha}+1})\left(\int_{0}^{z}\rho^{\frac{5}{\alpha}-1}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}d\rho\right)^{2}dz\right|

\displaystyle~~~+\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\frac{\alpha\beta}{|\alpha\beta-10\beta-4\alpha|}\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}\partial_{z}(z^{-\frac{10}{\alpha}-\frac{4}{\beta}+1})\left(\int_{0}^{z}\rho^{\frac{5}{\alpha}-1}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}d\rho\right)^{2}dz\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}\tilde{G}_{f}dz\right|+\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}z^{-\frac{4}{\beta}}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}\tilde{G}_{f}dz\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{G}_{f}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\|fw_{\theta}^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\tilde{G}_{f}z^{-\frac{2}{\beta}}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\|fz^{-\frac{2}{\beta}}w_{\theta}^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{G}_{f}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\|fw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}},

which directly implies that

\displaystyle\|\tilde{G}_{f}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\|fw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}.

(7.4)

Since
w^{\ast}_{z}\approx z^{-\frac{1}{4}}+z^{-\frac{1}{\beta}-\frac{1}{4}}
, it then follows from a similar way as (
7.4
) that

\displaystyle\|\tilde{G}_{f}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\|fw^{\ast,\eta}\|_{L^{2}}.

(7.5)

Since
D_{z}=z\partial_{z}
, one can get, by some straightforward computations, that

\displaystyle D_{z}\tilde{G}_{f}

\displaystyle=-\frac{15}{4\alpha^{2}}z^{-\frac{5}{\alpha}}\int_{0}^{z}\rho^{\frac{5}{\alpha}-1}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}d\rho+\frac{3}{4\alpha}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}

(7.6)

\displaystyle=-\frac{3}{4\alpha}z^{-\frac{5}{\alpha}}\int_{0}^{z}\partial_{\rho}(\rho^{\frac{5}{\alpha}})\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}d\rho+\frac{3}{4\alpha}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{4\alpha}z^{-\frac{5}{\alpha}}\int_{0}^{z}\rho^{\frac{5}{\alpha}-1}\langle D_{\rho}f,K\rangle_{\theta}d\rho

\displaystyle=\tilde{G}_{D_{z}f}.

The above equality implies that the operators
D_{z}
and
\tilde{G}
commute.
Following the same derivation procedure as in (
7.4
) and (
7.5
), we deduce from (
7.6
) that

\displaystyle\|D^{k}_{z}\tilde{G}_{f}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{k}},~~~~\|D^{k}_{z}\tilde{G}_{f}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{k,\ast}}.

(7.7)

It then follows from (
7.6
) that

\displaystyle\alpha D_{z}\tilde{G}_{f}

\displaystyle=-\frac{15}{4\alpha}z^{-\frac{5}{\alpha}}\int_{0}^{z}\rho^{\frac{5}{\alpha}-1}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}d\rho+\frac{3}{4}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}=-5\tilde{G}_{f}+\frac{3}{4}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta},

which, along with (
7.7
), gives that

\displaystyle\alpha\|D^{k+1}_{z}\tilde{G}_{f}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{k}},~~~~\alpha\|D^{k+1}_{z}\tilde{G}_{f}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\leq C\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{k,\ast}}.

(7.8)

The proof of Lemma
7.3
is thereby completed.
∎

As is well known, when considering the solutions of the Euler equations (
1
) in the
H^{3}
framework, by exploiting the divergence-free condition when dealing with the transport term, one can obtain that
\langle u\cdot\nabla u,u\rangle=0
. This is the so-called null structure.
Our next goal is to find suitable weight functions so that a similar null structure can be achieved.
More precisely, we have the following lemma.

Lemma 7.4
.

Let
f(z,\theta)|_{\partial D}=0
.
For any
\xi\in[0,+\infty)
, we denote that

\displaystyle\overline{w}^{\xi}\triangleq w^{\xi}z^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{3}{2\alpha}}(\cos\theta)^{-\frac{1}{2}},

(7.9)

then we have

\displaystyle\Big\langle\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\xi}}T_{\overline{w}^{\xi}f},(w^{\xi})^{2}f\Big\rangle=0.

(7.10)

Remark 7.5
.

In what follows, we mainly employ the cases
\xi=\eta
or
\lambda
from Lemma
7.4
.

The proof of Lemma
7.4
.

By virtue of integrating by parts, we deduce that

\displaystyle\Big\langle\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\xi}}T_{\overline{w}^{\xi}f},(w^{\xi})^{2}f\Big\rangle

\displaystyle=\Big\langle\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\xi}}T_{\overline{w}^{\xi}f},(\overline{w}^{\xi})^{2}fz^{\frac{3}{\alpha}-1}\cos\theta\Big\rangle

(7.11)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\big\langle T_{(\overline{w}^{\xi}f)^{2}},z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}-1}\cos\theta\big\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\big\langle(\overline{w}^{\xi}f)^{2},\partial_{\theta}(U(\Phi)z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}-1}\cos\theta)+\alpha\partial_{z}(V(\Phi)z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}}\cos\theta)\big\rangle.

Thanks to (
1.28
), we infer that

\displaystyle~~~~\partial_{\theta}(U(\Phi)z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}-1}\cos\theta)+\alpha\partial_{z}(V(\Phi)z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}}\cos\theta)

(7.12)

\displaystyle=\partial_{\theta}((-3\Phi-\alpha D_{z}\Phi)z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}-1}\cos\theta)+\alpha\partial_{z}((\partial_{\theta}\Phi-\tan\theta\Phi)z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}}\cos\theta)

\displaystyle=(-3\partial_{\theta}\Phi-\alpha\partial_{\theta}D_{z}\Phi)z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}-1}\cos\theta+(3\Phi+\alpha D_{z}\Phi)z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}-1}\sin\theta

\displaystyle~~~~+\alpha(\partial_{\theta}D_{z}\Phi-\tan\theta D_{z}\Phi)z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}-1}\cos\theta+3(\partial_{\theta}\Phi-\tan\theta\Phi)z^{\frac{3}{\alpha}-1}\cos\theta

\displaystyle=0.

We then substitute (
7.12
) into (
7.11
), to find that (
7.10
) holds.
Thus, we finish the proof of this lemma.
∎

7.2.  Transport estimates

Based on the elliptic estimates for equation (
6
) shown in Corollaries
6.17
,
6.18
and
6.20
, the transport null structure shown in Lemma
7.4
, we will give the
a priori
energy estimates for
\mathcal{H}^{-1}
-norm,
\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm and
\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm of
T_{g}
.

Proposition 7.6
.

Let
L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(0)=0
, there exist constants
\alpha>0
sufficiently small and
\eta(\beta)
, such that if
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
and
|\mu|\leq\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}
, then

\displaystyle\big|\langle T_{g},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle\big|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{10}\left(\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}\right)+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}},

(7.13)

\displaystyle\big|\langle T_{g},g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}\big|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{10}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}

(7.14)

\displaystyle+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)^{-2}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}},

\displaystyle\big|\langle T_{g},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}\big|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{10}(\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}+\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}})+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}})

(7.15)

\displaystyle+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\left(\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Proof.

We will divide this proof into three steps according to the definitions of different norms.

Step 1: Estimate for
\mathcal{H}^{-1}
-norm.

It is easy to check that the
\mathcal{H}^{-1}
-norm, which is defined by (
5.5
), can be decomposed as:

\displaystyle\langle T_{g},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle=\langle T_{g}^{l},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle+\langle T_{g}^{n},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{I}_{1}+\bar{I}_{2},

(7.16)

where
T^{l}_{g}
and
T^{n}_{g}
are defined by

\displaystyle T^{l}_{g}\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{U(\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}g+V\bigg(\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}g,

\displaystyle T^{n}_{g}\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}g+V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}g.

Now, we estimate the first term
\bar{I}_{1}
.
In view of (
3.18
), we have

\displaystyle L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})=\frac{2\alpha}{3}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}).

(7.17)

The preceding equality, combined with (
3.15
) and (
3.18
), necessarily implies that

\displaystyle\frac{U(\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3}{2}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})-\frac{\alpha}{2}D_{z}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})=-\frac{3}{2}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})+\frac{\alpha}{2}\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma},

(7.18)

\displaystyle V\bigg(\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)

\displaystyle=L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)).

(7.19)

Applying (
7.18
) and (
7.19
), we can simplify
T^{l}_{g}
to:

\displaystyle T^{l}_{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(-3L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})+\alpha\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\right)D_{\theta}g+L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})\left(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)\alpha D_{z}g.

(7.20)

Notice that

\displaystyle|w^{K}|\leq|w^{\eta}|\leq|w^{\lambda}|,

(7.21)

one gets, by employing (
3.15
) and (
7.20
), that

\displaystyle|\bar{I}_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}\right)\|gw^{K}\|_{L^{2}}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\alpha\|gw^{K}\|_{L^{2}}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}

(7.22)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(1+\frac{\alpha}{\beta}\right)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}+\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}(1-\eta)^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{100}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\frac{1}{100}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}.

Before estimating
\bar{I}_{2}
, we recall (
4.10
) and (
4.13
), to divide
\Phi
into the following six terms:

\displaystyle\Phi=\tilde{\Phi}_{g}+\tilde{G}_{g}\sin(2\theta)+G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta)+\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}+\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta)+G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta),

(7.23)

where for any function
f
(representing either
g
or
F_{\gamma}^{\ast}
),
\tilde{G}_{f}
and
G^{\ast}_{f}
are defined as

\displaystyle\tilde{G}_{f}(z)\triangleq\frac{3}{4\alpha}z^{-\frac{5}{\alpha}}\int_{0}^{z}\rho^{\frac{5}{\alpha}-1}\langle f,K\rangle_{\theta}d\rho,\quad G^{\ast}_{f}(z)=\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(f).

According to (
7.23
), we may split
\bar{I}_{2}
into the following two terms:

\displaystyle\bar{I}_{2}=\langle T_{g}^{n,1},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle+\langle T_{g}^{n,2},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{I}_{21}+\bar{I}_{22},

(7.24)

where
T_{g}^{n,1}
and
T_{g}^{n,2}
are defined by

\displaystyle T^{n,1}_{g}\triangleq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}+U(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\right)D_{\theta}g+V\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}+\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta)\right)\alpha D_{z}g

\displaystyle T^{n,2}_{g}\triangleq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}+U(\tilde{G}_{g})+U(G^{\ast}_{g})\right)D_{\theta}g+V\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{g}+\tilde{G}_{g}\sin(2\theta)+G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta)\right)\alpha D_{z}g.

With the help of Lemma
3.3
, Proposition
6.6
, Corollary
6.18
and Corollary
7.2
, we infer that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{\theta}U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(7.25)

In view of Lemma
3.3
, Lemma
7.1
and Lemma
7.3
, one obtains that

\displaystyle\|U(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|D_{z}U(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(7.26)

The application of Lemma
3.3
, Proposition
6.6
, Corollary
6.18
and Corollary
7.2
yields that

\displaystyle\|V(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{\theta}\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(7.27)

Again thanks to Lemma
3.3
, Lemma
7.1
and Lemma
7.3
, we see that

\displaystyle\|V(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|D_{z}\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(7.28)

Combining (
7.25
)-(
7.28
) together, we get, by using (
7.21
), that

\displaystyle|\bar{I}_{21}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}(\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}})\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.29)

According to Proposition
6.6
, Corollary
6.17
and Corollary
7.2
, we infer that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{\theta}U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\|\partial_{\theta}\tilde{\Phi}_{g}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha\|\partial_{\theta}D_{z}\tilde{\Phi}_{g}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.30)

By virtue of Lemma
7.1
and Lemma
7.3
, we get that

\displaystyle\|U(\tilde{G}_{g})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|D_{z}U(\tilde{G}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\|D_{z}\tilde{G}_{g}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}+\alpha\|D^{2}_{z}\tilde{G}_{g}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.31)

We can deduce from Lemma
7.1
that

\displaystyle\|U(G^{\ast}_{g})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|D_{z}U(G^{\ast}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|\langle g,K\rangle_{\theta}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}+\|\langle D_{z}g,K\rangle_{\theta}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.32)

By using Proposition
6.6
, Corollary
6.17
and Corollary
7.2
, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\|V(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{\theta}\tilde{\Phi}_{g}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.33)

We employ Lemma
7.1
and Lemma
7.3
once again, to find that

\displaystyle\|V(\tilde{G}_{g}\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D_{z}\tilde{G}_{g}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.34)

Taking advantage of Lemmas
7.1
, we get that

\displaystyle\|V(G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|D_{z}G^{\ast}_{g}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|\langle g,K\rangle_{\theta}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.35)

Then the combination of (
7.21
) and (
7.30
)-(
7.35
), gives that

\displaystyle|\bar{I}_{22}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}(\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}})\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.36)

Substituting (
7.29
) and (
7.36
) into (
7.24
), we achieve that

\displaystyle|\bar{I}_{2}|\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.37)

Plugging (
7.22
) and (
7.37
) into (
7.16
), one obtains that (
7.13
) holds.

Step 2: Estimate for
\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm.

We recall the definition of
\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}
in (
5.6
), to find that

\displaystyle\langle T_{g},g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}=

\displaystyle(1-\eta)^{2}\langle T_{g},g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle+\left[(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle D_{\theta}T_{g},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\right.

(7.38)

\displaystyle~~\left.+(1-\eta)^{2}\langle D_{z}T_{g},D_{z}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle\right]+\left[\langle D^{2}_{\theta}T_{g},D^{2}_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\left.~~+(1-\eta)^{4}\langle D^{2}_{z}T_{g},D^{2}_{z}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle+(1-\eta)^{2}\langle D_{z}D_{\theta}T_{g},D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{1}+\bar{J}_{2}+\bar{J}_{3}.

Here
\bar{J}_{1}
,
\bar{J}_{2}
and
\bar{J}_{3}
are related to the
\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\eta}
,
\dot{\mathcal{H}}^{1}_{\eta}
and
\dot{\mathcal{H}}^{2}_{\eta}
norms, respectively.

Step 2.1: Estimate for
\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm.

We can split
\mathcal{L}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm into the following two parts:

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{1}=(1-\eta)^{2}\langle T_{g}^{d},g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle-(1-\eta)^{2}\langle T_{g}^{e},g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{J}_{11}+\bar{J}_{12}.

(7.39)

Here
T^{d}_{g}
and
T^{e}_{g}
are defined by

\displaystyle T^{d}_{g}\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}\bigg[\frac{U(\Phi)}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}(g\overline{w}^{\eta})+V(\Phi)\alpha D_{z}(g\overline{w}^{\eta})\bigg]=\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}T_{g\overline{w}^{\eta}},

\displaystyle T^{e}_{g}\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}g\bigg[\frac{U(\Phi)}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}(\overline{w}^{\eta})+V(\Phi)\alpha D_{z}(\overline{w}^{\eta})\bigg]=\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}gT_{\overline{w}^{\eta}},

where
\overline{w}^{\eta}
is defined by (
7.9
) with
\xi=\eta
.
Whereas it follows from Lemma
7.4
with
\xi=\eta
that

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{11}=0.

(7.40)

Then by exactly the same procedure as that in the decomposition of (
7.16
), we can deduce from (
7.18
) and (
7.19
) that

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{12}=-(1-\eta)^{2}\langle T_{g}^{e,l},g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle-(1-\eta)^{2}\langle T_{g}^{e,n},g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{J}_{121}+\bar{J}_{122},

(7.41)

where
T^{e,l}_{g}
and
T^{e,n}_{g}
are defined by

\displaystyle T^{e,l}_{g}\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}g\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(-3L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})+\alpha\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\right)D_{\theta}\overline{w}^{\eta}+L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta))\alpha D_{z}\overline{w}^{\eta}\right],

\displaystyle T^{e,n}_{g}\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}g\left[\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}\overline{w}^{\eta}+V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}\overline{w}^{\eta}\right].

Recalling the definition of
\overline{w}^{\eta}
in (
7.9
), one can infer that

\displaystyle\frac{D_{\theta}\overline{w}^{\eta}}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}=

\displaystyle\frac{D_{\theta}w^{\eta}}{w^{\eta}}+\frac{D_{\theta}(\cos^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\theta))}{\cos^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\theta)}=-\eta\cos(2\theta)+\sin^{2}(\theta),

(7.42)

\displaystyle\frac{D_{z}\overline{w}^{\eta}}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}=

\displaystyle\frac{D_{z}w^{\eta}}{w_{\eta}}+\frac{D_{z}(z^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{3}{2\alpha}})}{z^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{3}{2\alpha}}}=-\frac{1}{\beta}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\beta})+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{3}{2\alpha}.

(7.43)

By applying (
7.42
) and (
7.43
), we simplify
T^{e,l}_{g}
as follows:

\displaystyle T^{e,l}_{g}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(-3L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})+\alpha\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\right)\left(-\eta\cos(2\theta)+\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)g

(7.44)

\displaystyle~~~+L^{-1}_{z}\left(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)\alpha\left(-\frac{1}{\beta}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\beta})+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{3}{2\alpha}\right)g

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha}{2}\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\left(-\eta\cos(2\theta)+\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)g-\frac{3(1-\eta)}{2}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})\cos(2\theta)g

\displaystyle~~~+\alpha L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})\left(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)\left(-\frac{1}{\beta}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\beta})+\frac{1}{2}\right)g.

This fact gives that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{121}|\lesssim\left(\frac{\alpha}{\beta}+(1-\eta)\right)(1-\eta)^{2}\|gw^{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{100}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}.

(7.45)

Recalling (
7.23
) and employing (
7.25
), (
7.26
) and (
7.30
)-(
7.32
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.46)

According (
7.23
), one can deduce from (
7.27
), (
7.28
) and (
7.33
)-(
7.35
) that

\displaystyle\bigg\|V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.47)

By virtue of (
7.42
) and (
7.43
), we infer that

\bigg\|\frac{D_{\theta}\overline{w}^{\eta}}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}+\alpha\bigg\|\frac{D_{z}\overline{w}^{\eta}}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C.

Combining this with (
7.46
) and (
7.47
), we obtain that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{122}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)(1-\eta)^{2}\|gw^{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.48)

We then substitute (
7.45
) and (
7.48
) into (
7.41
), to discover that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{12}|\leq\frac{1}{100}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.49)

Plugging (
7.40
) and (
7.49
) into (
7.39
), we find that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{1}|\leq\frac{1}{100}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.50)

Step 2.2: Estimate for
\dot{\mathcal{H}}^{1}_{\eta}
-norm.

Recalling (
7.38
), we split the
\dot{\mathcal{H}}^{1}_{\eta}
-norm into two terms as follows:

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{2}=(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle D_{\theta}T_{g},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle+(1-\eta)^{2}\langle D_{z}T_{g},D_{z}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{J}_{21}+\bar{J}_{22}.

(7.51)

We note that
\bar{J}_{21}
and
\bar{J}_{22}
are related to
D_{\theta}T_{g}
and
D_{z}T_{g}
, respectively.

Step 2.2.1: Estimate for the term related to
D_{\theta}T_{g}
.

We recall (
7.9
) with
\xi=\lambda
and using the similar way as (
7.39
), to divide
\bar{J}_{21}
into the following three parts:

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{21}=

\displaystyle(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle T_{D_{\theta}g},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle+(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle\mathcal{R}_{1},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

(7.52)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle T^{d}_{D_{\theta}g},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle-(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle T^{e}_{D_{\theta}g},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle+(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle\mathcal{R}_{1},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{211}+\bar{J}_{212}+\bar{J}_{213}.

Here
T^{d}_{D_{\theta}g}
,
T^{e}_{D_{\theta}g}
and the commutator
\mathcal{R}_{1}
are defined by

\displaystyle T^{d}_{D_{\theta}g}\triangleq\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\lambda}}T_{D_{\theta}g\overline{w}^{\lambda}},~~T^{e}_{D_{\theta}g}\triangleq\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\lambda}}D_{\theta}gT_{\overline{w}^{\lambda}},~~\mathcal{R}_{1}\triangleq D_{\theta}\left(\frac{U(\Phi)}{\sin(2\theta)}\right)D_{\theta}g+D_{\theta}V(\Phi)\alpha D_{z}g.

According to Lemma
7.4
, we have

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{211}=0.

(7.53)

As for
\bar{J}_{212}
, we deduce from a similar way as (
7.41
) that

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{212}=-(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle T^{e,l}_{D_{\theta}g},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle-(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle T^{e,n}_{D_{\theta}g},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{J}_{2121}+\bar{J}_{2122},

(7.54)

where
T^{e,l}_{D_{\theta}g}
and
T^{e,n}_{D_{\theta}g}
are defined by

\displaystyle T^{e,l}_{D_{\theta}g}\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\lambda}}D_{\theta}g\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(-3L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})+\alpha\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\right)D_{\theta}\overline{w}^{\lambda}+L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})\left(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)\alpha D_{z}\overline{w}^{\lambda}\right],

\displaystyle T^{e,n}_{D_{\theta}g}\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\lambda}}D_{\theta}g\left[\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}\overline{w}^{\lambda}+V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}\overline{w}^{\lambda}\right].

It then follows from the same derivation as (
7.42
) and (
7.43
) that

\displaystyle\frac{D_{\theta}\overline{w}^{\lambda}}{\overline{w}^{\lambda}}=-\lambda\cos(2\theta)+\sin^{2}(\theta),~~\frac{D_{z}\overline{w}^{\lambda}}{\overline{w}^{\lambda}}=-\frac{1}{\beta}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\beta})+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{3}{2\alpha}.

(7.55)

According to (
7.55
),
T^{e,l}_{D_{\theta}g}
can be simplified using a method similar to (
7.44
) as

\displaystyle T^{e,l}_{D_{\theta}g}=

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{2}\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\left(-\lambda\cos(2\theta)+\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)D_{\theta}g-\frac{3(1-\lambda)}{2}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})\cos(2\theta)D_{\theta}g

\displaystyle+\alpha L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta))\left(-\frac{1}{\beta}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\beta})+\frac{1}{2}\right)D_{\theta}g.

This, along with the facts that
\lambda=1+\frac{\alpha}{10\beta}
and
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
, yields that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{2121}|\lesssim\frac{\alpha}{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-2}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}.

(7.56)

By using (
7.55
), it is easy to check that

\bigg\|\frac{D_{\theta}\overline{w}^{\lambda}}{\overline{w}^{\lambda}}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}+\alpha\bigg\|\frac{D_{z}\overline{w}^{\lambda}}{\overline{w}^{\lambda}}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C,

which, together with (
7.46
) and (
7.47
), ensures that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{2122}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)(1-\eta)^{-2}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

(7.57)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)^{-2}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Substituting (
7.56
) and (
7.57
) into (
7.54
), we find that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{212}|\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)^{-2}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.58)

Now, we deal with
\bar{J}_{213}
.
Along the same line to the decomposition of (
7.16
), we can deduce from (
7.18
) and (
7.19
) that

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{213}=(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle\mathcal{R}_{1}^{l},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle+(1-\eta)^{-2}\langle\mathcal{R}_{1}^{n},D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{J}_{2131}+\bar{J}_{2132},

(7.59)

where
\mathcal{R}_{1}^{l}
and
\mathcal{R}_{1}^{n}
are defined by

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}^{l}\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2\alpha}L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})D_{\theta}(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta))\alpha D_{z}g=-3L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin^{2}(2\theta)\alpha D_{z}g,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}^{n}\triangleq

\displaystyle D_{\theta}\left[\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\right]D_{\theta}g+D_{\theta}\left[V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\right]\alpha D_{z}g.

One can deduce from (
3.15
) that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{2131}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha(1-\eta)^{-2}\|D_{z}g\sin^{2}(2\theta)w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}

(7.60)

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha(1-\eta)^{-2}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}.

According to (
7.23
), it follows from Proposition
6.6
, Corollary
6.17
, Corollary
6.18
and Corollary
7.2
, we infer that

\displaystyle~~~~\bigg\|D_{\theta}\bigg[\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg]\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}

(7.61)

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|D_{\theta}\bigg(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}+\bigg\|D_{\theta}\bigg(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{\theta}U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{\theta}U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\bigg\|\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}+\bigg\|\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\|\partial_{\theta}U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}+\|\partial_{\theta}U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Taking advantage of Lemma
3.3
, Proposition
6.6
, Corollary
6.17
, Corollary
6.18
and Corollary
7.2
, one obtains that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\theta}V(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}+\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}+\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}+\|\partial_{\theta}(\tan\theta(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}+\tilde{\Phi}_{g}))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\right)

(7.62)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

As the functions
\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}
,
\tilde{\Phi}_{g}
and
G^{\ast}_{g}
are independent of
\theta
, the same manners as (
7.28
), (
7.34
) and (
7.35
), yield immediately that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\theta}V((\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}+\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.63)

Combining (
7.62
) and (
7.63
) together, we have

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\theta}\bigg[V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.64)

The application of (
7.61
) and (
7.64
) gives that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{2132}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)(1-\eta)^{-2}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

(7.65)

\displaystyle~~~~+\left(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)(1-\eta)^{-2}\alpha\|D_{z}g\sin(2\theta)w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)^{-2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

We then substitute (
7.60
) and (
7.65
) into (
7.59
), to discover that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{213}|\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)^{-2}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.66)

Plugging (
7.53
), (
7.58
) and (
7.66
) into (
7.52
), we achieve that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{21}|\leq\frac{1}{100}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)^{-2}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.67)

Step 2.2.2: Estimate for the term related to
D_{z}T_{g}
.

Firstly, we split
\bar{J}_{22}
into two terms as follows:

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{22}=(1-\eta)^{2}\langle T_{D_{z}g},D_{z}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle+(1-\eta)^{2}\langle\mathcal{R}_{2},D_{z}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{J}_{221}+\bar{J}_{222},

(7.68)

where the commutator
\mathcal{R}_{2}
is defined by

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2}\triangleq D_{z}\left(\frac{U(\Phi)}{\sin(2\theta)}\right)D_{\theta}g+D_{z}V(\Phi)\alpha D_{z}g.

It then follows in a similar way to estimate (
7.50
) that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{221}|

\displaystyle=(1-\eta)^{2}\left|\left\langle\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}T_{D_{z}g\overline{w}^{\eta}}-\frac{1}{\overline{w}^{\eta}}D_{z}gT_{\overline{w}^{\eta}},D_{z}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\right\rangle\right|

(7.69)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\alpha}{\beta}+(1-\eta)\right)(1-\eta)^{2}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~+C_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)(1-\eta)^{2}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

A direct calculation yields directly that

\displaystyle D_{z}L_{z}^{-1}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})=-\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}.

(7.70)

By a similar decomposition of (
7.16
), one can deduce from (
7.18
), (
7.19
) and (
7.70
) that

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{222}=(1-\eta)^{2}\langle\mathcal{R}_{2}^{l},D_{z}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle+(1-\eta)^{2}\langle\mathcal{R}_{2}^{n},D_{z}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{J}_{2221}+\bar{J}_{2222},

(7.71)

where
\mathcal{R}_{2}^{l}
and
\mathcal{R}_{2}^{l}
are defined by

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2}^{l}

\displaystyle\triangleq\frac{1}{2}\left(3\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}+\alpha D_{z}\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\right)D_{\theta}g-\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\left(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)\alpha D_{z}g,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{2}^{n}

\displaystyle\triangleq D_{z}\left(\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\right)D_{\theta}g+D_{z}\bigg(V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}g.

In view of (
3.15
) and
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
, we find that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{2221}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\beta}(1-\eta)^{2}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{\alpha}{\beta}(1-\eta)^{2}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

(7.72)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\beta}(1-\eta)^{2}\left((1-\eta)^{2}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+(1-\eta)^{-2}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\right)+\frac{\alpha}{\beta}(1-\eta)^{2}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}.

Let us deal with
\bar{J}_{2222}
.
It follows in a similar way to (

\displaystyle\bigg\|D_{z}\left(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}\right)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|\partial_{\theta}U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(7.73)

According to Lemma
3.3
, Lemma
7.1
and Lemma
7.3
, we infer that

\displaystyle\|D_{z}U(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|D^{2}_{z}\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}+\alpha\|D^{3}_{z}\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(7.74)

While for the terms related to
g
, we can only controlled them by the

-norm for closing the energy estimates.

-norm.

With the help of Lemma

, we have

\displaystyle\|D_{z}U(\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}\lesssim\|D_{z}(\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}+\alpha\|D^{2}_{z}(\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.76)

Collecting (

)-(

), we find that

\displaystyle~~~~\left|(1-\eta)^{2}\bigg\langle D_{z}\bigg(\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)D_{\theta}g,D_{z}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\bigg\rangle\right|

(7.77)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{\frac{3}{2}}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{\alpha}(1-\eta)^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\|D_{\theta}gw_{\theta}^{\eta}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L^{2}_{\theta}}

Similar argument as (

(7.78)

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{2222}|

\displaystyle~~~~~~\|D^{2}_{z}U(\tilde{G}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}+\|D^{2}_{z}U(G^{\ast}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}+\bigg\|D^{2}_{z}\left(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}\right)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|D^{2}_{z}U(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{L^{\infty}}

(7.108)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{4,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

While by exactly the same procedure as that in the proof of (
7.107
), it is enough to show that

\displaystyle\bigg\|D^{2}_{z}\left(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\right)w_{z}\frac{w_{\theta}^{\eta}}{w_{\theta}^{\lambda}}\bigg\|_{L_{z}^{2}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.109)

Combining (
7.106
)-(
7.109
) together, we obtain that

\displaystyle~~~~(1-\eta)^{4}\bigg|\bigg\langle\bigg[D^{2}_{z},\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg]D_{\theta}g,D_{z}^{2}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(7.110)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{4}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|D^{2}_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|D_{z}D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{\theta}gw_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L^{2}_{\theta}}+\|D_{\theta}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

According to (
7.78
), Corollary
6.17
and Lemma
7.3
, we infer that

\displaystyle~~~~~\bigg\|D_{z}\bigg[V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\bigg]\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}

(7.111)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{\theta}D_{z}\tilde{\Phi}_{g}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|D^{2}_{z}\tilde{G}_{g}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}+\|D^{2}_{z}G^{\ast}_{g}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Along a similar way as (
7.78
)-(
7.80
), one gets that

\displaystyle~~~~\|D^{2}_{z}V\left(\tilde{\Phi}_{g}+(\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})\sin(2\theta)\right)w_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}+\|D^{2}_{z}V(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}+\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}}

(7.112)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

We then collect (
7.111
)-(
7.112
), to discover that

\displaystyle~~~~~(1-\eta)^{4}\left|\bigg\langle\bigg[D^{2}_{z},V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\bigg]\alpha D_{z}g,D^{2}_{z}g(w^{\eta})^{2}\bigg\rangle\right|

(7.113)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{4}\left(\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\|D^{2}_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~~\times\left(\|D^{2}_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{z}gw_{\theta}^{\eta}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L^{2}_{\theta}}+\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

According to (
7.110
) and (
7.113
), we deduce that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{3222}|\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.114)

We insert (
7.105
) and (
7.114
) into (
7.102
), to obtain that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{322}|\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.115)

Substituting (
7.101
) and (
7.115
) into (
7.100
), we finally get that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{32}|\leq\frac{1}{100}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.116)

Step 2.3.3: Estimate for the term related to
D_{z}D_{\theta}T_{g}
.

Firstly, we rewrite
D_{z}D_{\theta}T_{g}
as the following two terms:

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{33}=(1-\eta)^{2}\langle T_{D_{z}D_{\theta}g},D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle+(1-\eta)^{2}\langle\mathcal{R}_{5},D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{J}_{331}+\bar{J}_{332},

(7.117)

where the commutator
\mathcal{R}_{5}
is defined by

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{5}=\left[D_{z}D_{\theta},\frac{U(\Phi)}{\sin(2\theta)}\right]D_{\theta}g+\left[D_{z}D_{\theta},V(\Phi)\right]\alpha D_{z}g.

Along a similar way as (
7.50
), we deduce from Lemma
7.4
, (
7.46
) and (
7.47
) that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{331}|\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.118)

We control
\bar{J}_{332}
through a decomposition similar to (
7.59
), specifically:

\displaystyle\bar{J}_{332}=(1-\eta)^{2}\langle\mathcal{R}_{5}^{l},D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle+(1-\eta)^{2}\langle\mathcal{R}_{5}^{n},D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{J}_{3321}+\bar{J}_{3322},

(7.119)

where
\mathcal{R}_{5}^{l}
and
\mathcal{R}_{5}^{n}
are defined by

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{5}^{l}

\displaystyle\triangleq\frac{1}{2}\left(3\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}+\alpha D_{z}\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\right)D^{2}_{\theta}g-3\alpha\left(L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})D^{2}_{z}g-\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}D_{z}g\right)\sin^{2}(2\theta)-\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\left(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)\alpha D_{z}D_{\theta}g,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{5}^{n}

\displaystyle\triangleq\bigg[D_{z}D_{\theta},\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg]D_{\theta}g+\bigg[D_{z}D_{\theta},V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\bigg]\alpha D_{z}g.

Adapting the approach developed for (
7.60
) and (
7.72
), we derive

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{3321}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}.

(7.120)

By combining (
7.61
) and (
7.64
), and following a derivation analogous to (
7.65
), we establish that

\displaystyle~~~~(1-\eta)^{2}\bigg|\bigg\langle D_{\theta}\bigg(\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)D_{z}D_{\theta}g,D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(7.121)

\displaystyle~~~~+(1-\eta)^{2}\bigg|\bigg\langle D_{\theta}\left(V(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))\right)\alpha D^{2}_{z}g,D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Observing that
\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}
,
\tilde{G}_{g}
and
G^{\ast}_{g}
are independent of
\theta
, we derive through direct computations that

\displaystyle D_{z}D_{\theta}\bigg(\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)=D_{z}D_{\theta}\bigg(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)+D_{z}D_{\theta}\bigg(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg).

(7.122)

We deduce from Proposition
6.6
, Corollaries
6.17
,
6.18
and
7.2
that

\displaystyle\bigg\|D_{z}D_{\theta}\left(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}\right)w_{\theta}^{\lambda}\bigg\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L_{\theta}^{2}}\lesssim\|\partial_{\theta}U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}},

(7.123)

\displaystyle\bigg\|D_{z}D_{\theta}\left(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\right)w_{z}\bigg\|_{L_{z}^{2}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\left(\|\partial_{\theta}U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}\tilde{\Phi}_{g}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.124)

Combining (
7.122
)-(
7.124
) together, it comes out

\displaystyle~~~~(1-\eta)^{2}\bigg|\bigg\langle D_{z}D_{\theta}\bigg(\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)D_{\theta}g,D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(7.125)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|D_{\theta}gw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|D_{\theta}gw_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{\theta}}\right)\|D_{z}D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

We then deduce from the similar way as (
7.93
)-(
7.95
) that

\displaystyle\alpha\bigg\|D_{z}D_{\theta}V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)w_{\theta}^{\lambda}\bigg\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L_{\theta}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

By combining this with Corollary
7.2
, we obtain that

\displaystyle~~~~~(1-\eta)^{2}\bigg|\bigg\langle D_{z}D_{\theta}V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}g,D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(7.126)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\|D_{z}gw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\|D_{z}D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

By virtue of (
7.111
), it follows that

\displaystyle(1-\eta)^{2}\bigg|\bigg\langle D_{z}V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}D_{\theta}g,D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(7.127)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}

\displaystyle\left(\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Controlling
\|D_{z}\Big(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\Big)\|_{L^{\infty}}
within
\mathcal{H}^{2}
presents a fundamental difficulty. Our approach introduces the new norm of
D^{2}_{z}g
with an additional weight that avoids increasing regularity.
More precisely, we introduce the
\mathcal{E}^{2}
-norm.
According to Corollaries
7.2
,
6.20
and Lemma
6.2
, we infer that

\displaystyle\bigg\|D_{z}\bigg(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\bigg\|D^{2}_{z}D_{\theta}\bigg(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)w^{\lambda}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|\partial_{\theta}D^{2}_{z}U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

(7.128)

The application of (
7.106
) and (
7.128
), yields that

\displaystyle\bigg\|D_{z}\bigg(\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}},

(7.129)

which implies that

\displaystyle~~~~(1-\eta)^{2}\bigg|\bigg\langle D_{z}\bigg(\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)D^{2}_{\theta}g,D_{z}D_{\theta}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(7.130)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Taking advantage of (
7.121
), (
7.125
)-(
7.127
) and (
7.130
), it is easy to deduce that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{3322}|\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.131)

Substituting (
7.120
) and (
7.131
) into (
7.119
), we find that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{332}|\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.132)

We insert (
7.118
) and (
7.132
) into (
7.117
), to discover that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{33}|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{100}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.133)

We plug (
7.99
) in
Step 2.3.1
, (
7.116
) in
Step 2.3.2
and (
7.133
) in
Step 2.3.3
into (
7.86
), to find that

\displaystyle|\bar{J}_{3}|\leq\frac{3}{100}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.134)

Consequently, collecting (
7.50
) in
Step 2.1
, (
7.85
) in
Step 2.2
and (
7.134
) in
Step 2.3
, we get (
7.14
) immediately.

Step 3: Estimate for
\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm.

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}
in (
5.7
), we get that

\displaystyle\langle T_{g},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}=\alpha(1-\eta)^{2}\langle T_{g},g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle+\alpha(1-\eta)^{4}\langle D_{z}^{2}T_{g},D_{z}^{2}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{K}_{1}+\bar{K}_{2}.

(7.135)

By virtue of Lemma
7.4
, (
7.46
) and (
7.47
), we obtain that

\displaystyle|\bar{K}_{1}|\leq\frac{1}{400}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

(7.136)

We recall the definitions of
\mathcal{R}_{4}^{l}
and
\mathcal{R}_{4}^{n}
in
7.103
and (
7.104
), respectively, to find that

\displaystyle\bar{K}_{2}=

\displaystyle\alpha(1-\eta)^{4}\langle T_{D^{2}_{z}g},D_{z}^{2}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle+\alpha(1-\eta)^{4}\langle\mathcal{R}_{4}^{l},D_{z}^{2}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle+\alpha(1-\eta)^{4}\langle\mathcal{R}_{4}^{n},D_{z}^{2}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\rangle

(7.137)

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\bar{K}_{21}+\bar{K}_{22}+\bar{K}_{23}.

With the help of Lemma
7.4
, it follows from (
7.46
) and (
7.47
) that

\displaystyle|\bar{K}_{21}|\leq\frac{1}{200}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}+C_{\beta}(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

(7.138)

Along the similar way as (
7.105
), one can deduce from Lemma
6.2
that

\displaystyle|\bar{K}_{22}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\beta^{2}}(1-\eta)^{4}\alpha\|D^{2}_{z}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}(\|D_{\theta}D_{z}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}})

(7.139)

\displaystyle~~~+\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{2}}(1-\eta)^{4}\alpha(\|D^{2}_{z}gw^{\lambda}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|D_{z}gw^{\lambda}\|^{2}_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{200}(\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}+\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}).

By using the similar derivation as (
7.128
), we have

\displaystyle\bigg\|D^{2}_{z}\bigg(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)w_{z}\bigg\|_{L_{z}^{2}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

(7.140)

From this, (
7.108
) and (
7.129
), it follows that

\displaystyle~~~~~\alpha(1-\eta)^{4}\bigg|\bigg\langle\bigg[D^{2}_{z},\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg]D_{\theta}g,D^{2}_{z}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(7.141)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|D^{2}_{z}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|D_{z}D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{\theta}gw_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L^{2}_{\theta}}+\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Combining (
7.111
) and (
7.112
) together, we achieve that

\displaystyle~~~~~\alpha(1-\eta)^{4}\bigg|\bigg\langle[D^{2}_{z},V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)]\alpha D_{z}g,D^{2}_{z}g(w^{\lambda})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(7.142)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha\left(\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\|D^{2}_{z}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\left(\|D^{2}_{z}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{z}gw_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L^{2}_{\theta}}+\|D_{z}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

According to (
7.141
) and (
7.142
), we deduce that

\displaystyle|\bar{K}_{23}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

(7.143)

Substituting (
7.138
), (
7.139
) and (
7.143
) into (
7.137
), we finally get that

\displaystyle|\bar{K}_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{100}(\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}})+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

(7.144)

\displaystyle~~~+C_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

We then plug (
7.136
) and (
7.144
) into (
7.135
), to deduce (
7.15
) directly.

∎

7.3.  Product rules and transport estimates for fundamental solutions

In this subsection, we aim to establish the
a priori
energy estimates for
\mathcal{H}^{-1}
-norm,
\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm and
\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm of
T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}
.
Before we proceed, let us first present some fundamental lemmas concerning estimates related to
F^{\ast}_{\gamma}
.

Lemma 7.7
.

Assume that
f(z,\theta)|_{\partial D}=0
.
Let
\beta\in(0,1]
.
There exists a sufficiently small constant
\alpha>0
, such that if
\alpha\ll\beta
and
|\mu|\leq\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}
, then there holds

\displaystyle\|fD_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}},~~~~\|fD_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{4}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}},

(7.145)

and

\displaystyle\|fD_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}},~~~~\|fD_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{4}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.146)

Proof.

Recalling (
3.19
), it is easy to check that for any
1\leq k\leq 3
,

\displaystyle|D^{k}_{z}\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\gamma^{k}}\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma},

(7.147)

\displaystyle|D_{\theta}\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}|\lesssim\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma},~~~~

\displaystyle|D^{2}_{\theta}\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}|+|D^{3}_{\theta}\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}|\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}+\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}\sin(2\theta).

(7.148)

In view of (
3.19
) and (
4.23
), we have

\displaystyle\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}\leq 1,~~~~\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}w_{z}\lesssim\frac{1}{\gamma}w^{\ast}_{z}.

(7.149)

This, combined with (
3.18
) and (
7.148
), establishes that

\displaystyle\|fD_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\gamma}\|f(\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\eta})(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}w_{z})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\|fw_{\theta}^{\eta}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\|f\|_{H^{2,\ast}}.

(7.150)

Using (
3.18
), (
7.147
)-(
7.149
), with arguments similar to (
7.150
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\|D_{z}(fD_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma})w^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{2}_{z}(fD_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma})w^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\|f\|_{H^{2,\ast}}.

(7.151)

While for terms related to
D_{\theta}f
, we can simply handle them by employing (
3.18
), (
7.148
) and (
7.149
), for example:

\displaystyle\|D_{\theta}fD_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\gamma}\|D_{\theta}f(\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda})(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}w_{z})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\|D_{\theta}fw_{\theta}^{\lambda}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\|f\|_{H^{2,\ast}}.

(7.152)

Similar argument leads us to get that

\displaystyle\|D_{\theta}fD^{2}_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{2}_{\theta}fD_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\|f\|_{H^{2,\ast}}.

(7.153)

Again thanks to (
3.18
), (
7.148
)-(
7.149
), we can deduce from the same way that

\displaystyle\|D_{\theta}fD_{z}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{z}D_{\theta}fD_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{3}}\|f\|_{H^{2,\ast}}.

(7.154)

In view of (
3.18
), (
3.19
), (
7.148
) and Corollary
7.2
, one obtains that

\displaystyle\|fD^{2}_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{3}}{\gamma^{2}}\|f(\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda})(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}w_{z})\|_{L^{2}}+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\gamma}\|f(\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\eta})(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}w_{z})\|_{L^{2}}

(7.155)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{3}}{\gamma^{3}}\|fw^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}\|\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{\theta}^{2}}+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\|fw_{\theta}^{\eta}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\|f\|_{H^{2,\ast}},

where we have used the fact that

\displaystyle\|\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{\theta}^{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\gamma}{\alpha}}.

(7.156)

By applying (
3.18
), (
7.147
)-(
7.149
), and following a derivation analogous to (
7.155
), we establish that

\displaystyle\|fD^{3}_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{2}}\|f\|_{H^{2,\ast}},

(7.157)

\displaystyle\|D_{z}fD^{2}_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|fD_{z}D^{2}_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{3}}\|f\|_{H^{2,\ast}}.

(7.158)

Combining (
7.150
)-(
7.158
) together, we finally obtain that the first inequality in (
7.145
) holds.
Similarly, the other three inequalities can be derived directly.
Thus, we complete the proof of Lemma
7.7
.
∎

In comparison with Lemma
7.7
, we consider the case where the function
f(z)
is independent of
\theta
, yielding the following similar lemma.

Lemma 7.8
.

Assume that
f(z)
is independent of
\theta
and satisfies
f(0)=0
.
Let
\beta\in(0,1]
.
There exist constants
\alpha>0
sufficiently small and
\eta(\beta)
, such that if
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
and
|\mu|\leq\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}
, then

\displaystyle\|fD_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{4}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}fw_{z}^{\ast}\|_{L^{2}_{z}},~~~~\|fD_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{4}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}fw_{z}^{\ast}\|_{L^{2}_{z}},

\displaystyle\|fD_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{4}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}fw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}},~~~~\|fD_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{4}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}fw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}.

Proof.

By virtue of (
3.18
), (
7.147
) and (
7.149
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\|fD_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}\|f(\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\eta})(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}w_{z})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha}{\gamma^{2}}\|fw^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}\|w_{\theta}^{\eta}\|_{L_{\theta}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|f(z)w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}.

(7.159)

With the help of (
7.147
)-(
7.149
), similar argument leads us to get that

\displaystyle\|D_{z}(fD_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma})w^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{2}_{z}(fD_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma})w^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{4}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}fw_{z}^{\ast}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}.

(7.160)

Again thanks to (
3.18
), (
7.147
)-(
7.149
) and (
7.156
), one can deduce that

\displaystyle\|fD_{z}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\gamma^{2}}\|f(\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda})(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}w_{z})\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\gamma^{3}}\|fw^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}\|\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{\theta}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{2}}\|fw_{z}^{\ast}\|_{L^{2}_{z}},

(7.161)

where we have used the fact that
\alpha\ll\beta
.
Along the similar way as (
7.161
), it follows that

\displaystyle\|fD_{z}D^{2}_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{2}}\|fw_{z}^{\ast}\|_{L^{2}_{z}},

(7.162)
