\displaystyle\|D_{z}fD_{z}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|fD^{2}_{z}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{3}}\sum_{i=0}^{1}\|D^{i}_{z}fw_{z}^{\ast}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}.

(7.163)

Combining (
7.159
)-(
7.163
), we finally obtain the second inequality in Lemma
7.7
. The proofs of the other three inequalities can be obtained by the same method.
We thereby complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

With Lemmas
3.3
,
7.7
and
7.8
at hand, we are ready to establish the
a priori
energy estimates for
\mathcal{H}^{-1}
-norm,
\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm and
\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm of
T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}
.

Proposition 7.9
.

Let
L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(0)=0
, and
\beta\in(0,1]
, there exist constants
\alpha>0
sufficiently small and
\eta(\beta)
, such that if
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
and
|\mu|\leq\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}
, then

\displaystyle|\langle T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right),

(7.164)

\displaystyle|\langle T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}},g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\left(\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right),

(7.165)

\displaystyle|\langle T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\right).

(7.166)

Proof.

We will divide this proof into three steps.

Step 1: Estimate for
\mathcal{H}^{-1}
-norm.

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{H}^{-1}
-norm in (
5.5
), we decompose it as follows:

\displaystyle\langle T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle=\langle T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}^{l},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle+\langle T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}^{n},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle\triangleq\bar{L}_{1}+\bar{L}_{2}.

(7.167)

Here
T^{l}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}
and
T^{n}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}
are defined by

\displaystyle T^{l}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\frac{U(\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}+V\bigg(\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},

\displaystyle T^{n}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}+V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}.

With the help of (
3.15
), (
3.18
), (
7.18
) and (
7.19
), one can simplify
T^{l}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}
to:

\displaystyle T^{l}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(-3L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})+\alpha\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\right)\frac{2\alpha}{3\gamma}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\left(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)

(7.168)

\displaystyle+L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})\left(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}(L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})-1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}\left(\frac{\alpha}{3}-2\gamma\right)F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\left(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right).

This, along with the fact that
|w^{K}|\leq|w^{\eta}|
, Lemma
3.3
and (
7.149
), yields directly that

\displaystyle|\bar{L}_{1}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\|T^{l}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.169)

As for
\bar{L}_{2}
, by virtue of Lemmas
3.3
,
7.7
and
7.8
, we deduce that

\displaystyle~~~~\bigg|\bigg\langle\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g(w^{K})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(7.170)

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\bigg\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\left(\|D^{i}_{z}U(\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}+\|D^{i}_{z}U(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})w_{z}^{\ast}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\right)

\displaystyle~~~~+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\left(\bigg\|\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\bigg\|\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{3}(1-\eta)^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Note that

\displaystyle V(f(z)\sin(2\theta))=2f(z)(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)),\quad D_{\theta}(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta))=-3\sin^{2}(2\theta).

These two estimates, combined with Lemma
3.3
and Lemma
7.7
, establish that

\displaystyle~~~~\bigg|\bigg\langle V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g(w^{K})^{2}\bigg\rangle\bigg|

(7.171)

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha\bigg\|V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\bigg\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\left(\|D^{i}_{z}(\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}+\|D^{i}_{z}\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\right)

\displaystyle~~~~+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}(\|V(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|V(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{3}(1-\eta)^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Combining (
7.170
) and (
7.171
) together, we find that

\displaystyle|\bar{L}_{2}|\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{3}(1-\eta)^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.172)

Substituting (
7.169
) and (
7.172
) into (
7.167
), then using the fact that
\alpha\ll(1-\eta)
, one gets (
7.164
) immediately.

Step 2: Estimate for
\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm.

We recall the definition of
\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm in (
5.6
), to obtain that

\displaystyle\langle T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}},g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}=\langle T^{l}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}},g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+\langle T^{n}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}},g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}\triangleq\bar{M}_{1}+\bar{M}_{2}.

(7.173)

It follows from the similar way as (
7.169
) that

\displaystyle|\bar{M}_{1}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\|T^{l}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}\lesssim(1-\eta)^{-2}\|T^{l}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.174)

By employing a similar derivation procedure as in (
7.170
), we directly deduce that

\displaystyle~~~~\bigg|\bigg\langle\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g\bigg\rangle_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}\bigg|

(7.175)

\displaystyle\lesssim(1-\eta)^{-2}\bigg\|\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\bigg\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{3}(1-\eta)^{-3}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

The similar derivation to (
7.171
) directly yields that

\displaystyle~~~~\bigg|\bigg\langle V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g\bigg\rangle_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}\bigg|

(7.176)

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha(1-\eta)^{-2}\bigg\|V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\bigg\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{3}(1-\eta)^{-3}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

We collect (
7.175
) and (
7.176
) together, to achieve that

\displaystyle|\bar{M}_{2}|\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{3}(1-\eta)^{-3}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(7.177)

Since
\alpha\ll(1-\eta)
, we insert (
7.174
) and (
7.177
) into (
7.173
), to get that (
7.165
) holds.

Step 3: Estimate for
\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm.

We remember the definition of
\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm in (
5.7
), to find that

\displaystyle\langle T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}=\langle T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}^{l},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}+\langle T_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}^{n},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}\triangleq\bar{N}_{1}+\bar{N}_{2}.

(7.178)

In view of (
7.147
), (
7.156
) and (
7.168
), we have

\displaystyle|\bar{N}_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|T^{l}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\|_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}\lesssim\alpha\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{5}{2}}\|\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w^{\lambda}_{\theta}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

(7.179)

By using (
7.156
), Lemma
6.2
, Corollary
6.19
and Lemma
7.3
, we see that

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg\langle\frac{U(\Phi_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g\bigg\rangle_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|\frac{U(\Phi_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\bigg\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

(7.180)

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\beta^{4}}\bigg\|\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\|U(\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\right)\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\left(\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}U(\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}\|\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{\theta}^{2}}\right)\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\left(\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}},

where
\Phi_{g}\triangleq\tilde{\Phi}_{g}+(\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})\sin(2\theta)
.
Thanks to (
7.156
), Lemmas
3.3
,
6.2
and
7.3
, one can deduce that

\displaystyle~~~~\bigg|\bigg\langle\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g\bigg\rangle_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}\bigg|+\left|\langle U(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}\right|

(7.181)

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\bigg\|\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\bigg\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\|U(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\bigg)\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\left(\bigg\|D^{i}_{z}\bigg(\frac{U(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})}{\sin(2\theta)}\bigg)w_{z}^{\ast}\bigg\|_{L_{z}^{2}L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|D_{z}^{i}U(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\left(\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{H^{3,\ast}}+\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{H^{2,\ast}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{5}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

The application of (
7.156
), Lemma
6.2
, Corollary
6.19
and Lemma
7.3
, yields directly that

\displaystyle|\langle V(\Phi_{g})\alpha D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha\|V(\Phi_{g})D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

(7.182)

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\left(\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{4}}\|V(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\|V((\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})\sin(2\theta))D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\left(\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}(\tilde{G}_{g}+G^{\ast}_{g})w_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}\|\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{\theta}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}(\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}},

and

\displaystyle~~~~\left|\langle V(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\alpha D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}\right|+\left|\langle V(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))\alpha D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}\right|

(7.183)

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\left(\|V(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\|V(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\left(\|D^{i}_{z}V(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})w_{z}^{\ast}\|_{L_{z}^{2}L^{\infty}_{\theta}}+\|D_{z}^{i}\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L_{z}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{H^{3,\ast}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{5}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

The combination of (
7.180
)-(
7.183
), yields that

\displaystyle|\bar{N}_{2}|\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{5}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

(7.184)

Plugging (
7.179
)and (
7.184
) into (
7.178
), we finally get (
7.166
) immediately.
∎

8
Blow-up phenomenon of the Euler equations

Firstly, we rewrite
F
as a perturbation of
F^{\ast}_{\gamma}
, namely,
F=F^{\ast}_{\gamma}+g
.
Then we recall that
g
satisfies

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\mathcal{L}_{\Gamma}(g)=-T+R_{0}+R_{1}+R_{2},\\[4.30554pt]
L^{\alpha}_{z}(\Phi)+L_{\theta}(\Phi)=F,\\[4.30554pt]
\mu=\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(R_{2}-T)(0),~~\gamma=\frac{1+\mu}{1-\mu}\beta,\\[4.30554pt]
L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(0)=0,~~g|_{\partial D}=0,~~\Phi|_{\partial D}=0,\end{array}\right.

where the operator
\mathcal{L}_{\Gamma}
and the remaining terms
R_{i}

(i=0,1,2)
are defined in (
1.35
)-(
1.37
), respectively.
We have obtained the coercivity estimate of the operator
\mathcal{L}_{\Gamma}
in Section
5
, the elliptic estimates of the solution
\Phi
in Section
6
, and the estimates of the transport term
T
in Section
7
.
With all these estimates at hand, we intend to prove our main theorems in this section.

8.1.  Final a priori estimate

As observed in the first equation of (
8
), all terms except
R_{0}
,
R_{1}
and
R_{2}
have been rigorously addressed in Sections
5
-
7
.
We begin by estimating
R_{2}
.
Before proceeding, we establish some fundamental lemmas.

Lemma 8.1
.

Let
f(z,\theta)|_{\partial D}=g(z,\theta)|_{\partial D}=0
. Then we have

\displaystyle\|fg\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}},~~~\|fg\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(8.5)

Proof.

By using Corollary
7.2
, it is easy to verify that the first inequality of (
8.5
) holds true.
It remains to prove the second one.
By virtue of Corollary
7.2
, we infer that

\displaystyle\|fgw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\|gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\|D_{z}(fg)w^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{z}fw_{\theta}^{\eta}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L_{\theta}^{2}}\|gw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}},

\displaystyle\|D_{\theta}(fg)w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{\theta}fw_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L_{\theta}^{2}}\|gw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}.

Along the similar way, one obtains that

\displaystyle\|D^{2}_{\theta}(fg)w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{2}_{z}(fg)w^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|D^{2}_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{2}_{\theta}fw_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L_{\theta}^{2}}\|gw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}+\|D_{\theta}fw_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L_{\theta}^{2}}\|D_{\theta}gw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|D^{2}_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{2}_{z}fw_{\theta}^{\eta}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L_{\theta}^{2}}\|gw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}+\|D_{z}fw_{\theta}^{\eta}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L_{\theta}^{2}}\|D_{z}gw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\|D_{\theta}D_{z}(fg)w^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|D_{\theta}D_{z}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{\theta}D_{z}fw_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L_{\theta}^{2}}\|gw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}

\displaystyle~~~+\|D_{\theta}fw_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L_{z}^{\infty}L_{\theta}^{2}}\|D_{z}gw_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L_{\theta}^{\infty}}+\|D_{z}f\|_{L^{\infty}}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

We thus complete the proof of Lemma
8.1
.
∎

Applying the similar way as the proof of Lemma
7.7
, we can get the following lemma directly. And its proof is omitted here.

Lemma 8.2
.

Assume that
f(z,\theta)|_{\partial D}=0
.
Let
\beta\in(0,1]
.
There exists a sufficiently small constant
\alpha>0
, such that if
\alpha\ll\beta
and
|\mu|\leq\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}
, then there holds

\displaystyle\|fF^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{3}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}},~~~~\|fF^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\leq C\frac{\alpha}{\beta^{3}}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}.

Based on Lemmas
8.1
-
8.2
, we now proceed to deal with
R_{2}
by using the product rules.

Proposition 8.3
.

Let
L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(0)=0
. There exist constants
\alpha>0
sufficiently small and
\eta(\beta)
, such that if
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
and
|\mu|\leq\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}
, then

\displaystyle|\langle R_{2},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-1}(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}),

(8.6)

\displaystyle|\langle R_{2},g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-3}(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}),

(8.7)

\displaystyle|\langle R_{2},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}).

(8.8)

Proof.

This proof will be divided into three steps.

Step 1: Estimate for
\mathcal{H}^{-1}
-norm.

Recalling the definition of
R_{2}
in (
1.37
) and
F=F^{\ast}_{\gamma}+g
, we can decompose the
\mathcal{H}^{-1}
-norm as:

\displaystyle\langle R_{2},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{2\alpha}\langle L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)g,g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle-\frac{3}{2}\langle\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle F,K\rangle_{\theta}F,g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle

(8.9)

\displaystyle~~~+\langle\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{g}-G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta))g,g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle+\langle\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}-G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))g,g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~+\langle\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{g}-G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta))F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle+\langle\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}-G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))F^{\ast}_{\gamma},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{i=1}^{6}\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{i},

where for
f
being
g
or
F^{\ast}_{\gamma}
, the notation
\Phi_{f}
is defined as
\Phi_{f}\triangleq\tilde{\Phi}_{f}+\tilde{G}_{f}\sin(2\theta)+{G}_{f}^{\ast}\sin(2\theta)
.
We now estimate
\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{i}

(i=1,\cdots,6)
term by term.
Firstly, since
L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)
is dependent of
\theta
, we then deduce from Lemma
7.1
that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{1}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\alpha^{-1}\|L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)w_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(8.10)

By applying Lemmas
3.3
,
8.1
and
8.2
, we find that
\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{2}
can be controlled by

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\left(\|\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle F,K\rangle_{\theta}g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle F,K\rangle_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right)

(8.11)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\left(\|\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle g,K\rangle_{\theta}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle F^{\ast}_{\gamma},K\rangle_{\theta}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}\right)

\displaystyle~~~~+\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\left(\|\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle g,K\rangle_{\theta}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle F^{\ast}_{\gamma},K\rangle_{\theta}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-1}\left(\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right),

where we have used the fact that

\displaystyle\|\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle F^{\ast}_{\gamma},K\rangle_{\theta}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{k,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-1}.

(8.12)

We employ Corollary
6.17
and Lemma
7.3
, to obtain that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{G}_{g}\sin(2\theta))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

(8.13)

This estimate, together with Lemma
8.1
, yields directly that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{g}-G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta))g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}

(8.14)

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{G}_{g}\sin(2\theta))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

With the help of Lemma
3.3
, Corollary
6.18
and Lemma
7.3
, we achieve that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(8.15)

Combining the estimate above and Lemma
8.1
together, it is enough to prove that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}-G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}

(8.16)

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}})\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

The application of Lemma
8.2
and (
8.13
), gives that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{5}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{g}-G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta))F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}

(8.17)

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha(\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{\Phi}_{g})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{G}_{g}\sin(2\theta))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

By virtue of Lemma
8.2
and (
8.15
), one gets that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{I}}_{6}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}-G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}

(8.18)

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha(\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{\Phi}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}})\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\tilde{G}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2,\ast}})\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

Substituting (
8.10
)-(
8.18
) into (
8.9
), we finally obtain (
8.6
).

Step 2: Estimate for
\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm.

It is easy to check that

\displaystyle\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}\lesssim(1-\eta)^{-1}\|f\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}.

We employ the estimate above and the definition of
R_{2}
in (
1.37
), then use a similar derivation as
\bar{\mathcal{I}_{i}}

(i=1,\cdots,6)
in (
8.10
)-(
8.18
), finally deduce (
8.7
) immediately.

Step 3: Estimate for
\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm.

We remember the definition of
R_{2}
in (
1.37
) and
F=F^{\ast}_{\gamma}+g
, to decompose the
\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}
-norm as follows:

\displaystyle\langle R_{2},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{2\alpha}\langle L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)g,g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}-\frac{3}{2}\langle\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle F,K\rangle_{\theta}F,g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}+\langle\mathcal{R}(\Phi-G^{\ast}_{F}\sin(2\theta))F,g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}

(8.19)

\displaystyle\triangleq\sum_{i=1}^{3}\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{i},

We now estimate
\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{i}

(i=1,2,3)
in turn.
At first, we apply Lemma
7.1
, to discover that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{1}|\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)w_{z}\|_{L^{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

(8.20)

Again thanks to Lemma
7.1
, it then follows from Lemma
8.1
and Lemma
8.2
that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}(\|\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle F,K\rangle_{\theta}g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\|\sin^{2}(\theta)\langle F,K\rangle_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}})

(8.21)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\bigg(\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|\langle D^{i}_{z}g,K\rangle_{\theta}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{j=0}^{3}\|\langle D^{j}_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},K\rangle_{\theta}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle~~~~+\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\left(\|\langle D^{i}_{z}g,K\rangle_{\theta}w_{z}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\langle D^{i}_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma},K\rangle_{\theta}w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

With the help of (
8.13
), (
8.15
), Corollaries
6.18
,
6.20
and Lemmas
7.1
,
7.3
,
8.1
,
8.2
, one obtains that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{J}}_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim(1-\eta)^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\left(\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi-G^{\ast}_{F}\sin(2\theta))g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi-G^{\ast}_{F}\sin(2\theta))F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\right)

(8.22)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{g}-G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{g}-G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta))\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{2}\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}-G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))\|_{\mathcal{H}^{4,\ast}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\Big(\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{g}-G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta))\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{2}\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}-G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\|\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}w_{\theta}^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}},

where we have used the fact that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{2}\|D^{i}_{z}\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}-G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{L^{2}_{z}L^{\infty}_{\theta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|D^{i}_{z}\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}-G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))w^{\ast}_{z}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{3,\ast}}\lesssim\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

Plugging (
8.22
)-(
8.20
) into (
8.19
), we conclude that (
8.8
) holds.

∎

It remains to address
R_{0}
and
R_{1}
in the first equation of (
8
).
Noting that
\mu
is present in both terms, we first derive the following lemma concerning the estimation of
\mu
.

Proposition 8.4
.

Let
L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(0)=0
. There exist constants
\alpha>0
sufficiently small and
\eta(\beta)
, such that if
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
and
|\mu|\leq\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}
, then

\displaystyle|\mu|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}+C_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha\right).

(8.23)

Proof.

Recalling the definition in
\eqref{8eq1}_{3}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mu=-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(T)(0)+\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(R_{2})(0)\triangleq\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{1}+\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{2}.

(8.24)

In order to estimate
\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{1}
, we decompose it as the following two terms:

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{1}=-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(T^{l})(0)-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(T^{n})(0)\triangleq\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{11}+\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{12},

(8.25)

where
T^{l}
and
T^{n}
are defined by

\displaystyle T^{l}

\displaystyle\triangleq\frac{U(\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F+V\bigg(\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}F,

\displaystyle T^{n}

\displaystyle\triangleq\frac{U(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta))}{\sin(2\theta)}D_{\theta}F+V\bigg(\Phi-\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\sin(2\theta)\bigg)\alpha D_{z}F.

Applying (
7.18
), (
7.19
), (
7.168
) and the fact that
F=F^{\ast}_{\gamma}+g
, one can simplify
T^{l}
as follows:

\displaystyle T^{l}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}(-3L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})+\alpha\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})D_{\theta}g+L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})\left(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right)\alpha D_{z}g

(8.26)

\displaystyle~~~+\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}\left(\frac{\alpha}{3}-2\gamma\right)F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\left(\cos(2\theta)-\sin^{2}(\theta)\right).

It is easy to verify that for
\xi=\eta
or
\lambda
,

\displaystyle\|K(\theta)z^{-1}(w^{\xi})^{-1}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|K(\theta)(w^{\xi}_{\theta})^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}\|z^{-1}w_{z}^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim 1.

(8.27)

We employ (
8.26
) and (
8.27
), to discover that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{11}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha^{-1}|\langle T^{l},K(\theta)z^{-1}\rangle|

(8.28)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\langle|D_{\theta}g|+\alpha|D_{z}g|,K(\theta)z^{-1}\rangle+\langle F^{\ast}_{\gamma},K(\theta)z^{-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|D_{\theta}gw^{\lambda}\|_{L^{2}}\|K(\theta)z^{-1}(w^{\lambda})^{-1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D_{z}gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\|K(\theta)z^{-1}(w^{\eta})^{-1}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\alpha\|K(\theta)\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}\|\Gamma_{\gamma}^{\ast}z^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha.

Recalling (
7.46
) and (
7.47
) and (
8.27
), it is enough to prove that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{12}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha^{-1}|\langle T^{n},K(\theta)z^{-1}\rangle|

(8.29)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\langle|D_{\theta}F|+\alpha|D_{z}F|,K(\theta)z^{-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})(\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha,

where we have used the fact that
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
and

\displaystyle\langle|D_{\theta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}|+\alpha|D_{z}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}|,K(\theta)z^{-1}\rangle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{2}.

Substituting (
8.28
) and (
8.29
) into (
8.25
), we find that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{1}|\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}^{2}+\alpha\right).

(8.30)

As for
\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{2}
, we deduce from the definition of
R_{2}
in (
1.37
) that

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{9}{8\alpha^{2}}L^{-1}_{z,K}\big(L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)g\big)(0)-\frac{9}{8\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}\big((\sin\theta)^{2}\langle F,K\rangle_{\theta}F\big)(0)

(8.31)

\displaystyle~~~~+\frac{3}{4\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(\mathcal{R}(\Phi-G^{\ast}_{F}\sin(2\theta))F)(0)

\displaystyle\triangleq\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{21}+\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{22}+\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{23}.

After some direct calculations, we have

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{21}=-\frac{9}{8\alpha^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)\partial_{z}L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)dz=\frac{9}{16\alpha^{2}}\big(L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(0)\big)^{2}=0.

(8.32)

Along a similar way as (
8.27
), we see that for
\xi=\eta
or
\lambda
,

\displaystyle\|K(\theta)(w^{\xi}_{\theta})^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}\lesssim 1.

This, along with Lemma
7.1
and (
8.27
), yields that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{22}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|\langle|F|,K(\theta)\rangle_{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}}\langle|F|,K(\theta)z^{-1}\rangle

(8.33)

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\left(\|gw^{\eta}_{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}L^{2}_{\theta}}\|K(\theta)(w^{\eta}_{\theta})^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}+\alpha\|K(\theta)\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}\|\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{z}}\right)

\displaystyle~~~\times\left(\|gw^{\eta}\|_{L^{2}}\|K(\theta)(w^{\eta})^{-1}\|_{L^{2}}+\alpha\|K(\theta)\Gamma_{\theta,\gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}\|\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma}z^{-1}\|_{L^{2}_{z}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(\frac{\alpha}{\beta}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha.

We use the similar derivation as (
7.46
), (
7.47
) and (
8.28
), to get that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi-G^{\ast}_{F}\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\langle|\mathcal{R}(\Phi-G^{\ast}_{F}\sin(2\theta))|,K(\theta)z^{-1}\rangle\lesssim_{\beta}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

Applying these two estimates above and using the similar way as (
8.28
), one obtains that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{23}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi-G^{\ast}_{F}\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}}\langle|g|,K(\theta)z^{-1}\rangle

(8.34)

\displaystyle~~~+\alpha^{-1}\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}}\langle|\mathcal{R}(\Phi-G^{\ast}_{F}\sin(2\theta))|,K(\theta)z^{-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

Plugging (
8.32
), (
8.33
) and (
8.34
) into (
8.31
), we achieve that

\displaystyle|\bar{\mathcal{K}}_{2}|\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(8.35)

We then insert (
8.30
) and (
8.35
) into (
8.24
), to conclude that (
8.23
) holds.
∎

With the help of Proposition
8.4
, we can give the following proposition concerning the estimates of
R_{0}
and
R_{1}
.

Proposition 8.5
.

Let
L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(0)=0
. There exist constants
\alpha>0
sufficiently small and
\eta(\beta)
, such that if
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
and
|\mu|\leq\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}
, then

\displaystyle|\langle R_{0}+R_{1},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}

(8.36)

\displaystyle~~~+C_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{4}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle|\langle R_{0}+R_{1},g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}^{2}

(8.37)

\displaystyle~~~+C_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\left(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{4}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle|\langle R_{0}+R_{1},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}+C_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\right)\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

(8.38)

\displaystyle~~~+C_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha)\right)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

Proof.

We apply Proposition
8.4
and (
4.24
), to deduce that

\displaystyle|\langle R_{0},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|R_{0}w^{K}\|_{L^{2}}\|gw^{K}\|_{L^{2}}

(8.39)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\Big(\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha\big)\Big)\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}(1+z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}})^{-1}w^{K}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}}\|gw^{K}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}).

Integrating by parts and employing Proposition
8.4
, we get that

\displaystyle|\langle R_{1},g(w^{K})^{2}\rangle|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha\right)\right)\|gw^{K}\|_{\mathcal{L}^{2}}^{2}

(8.40)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}^{3}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{4}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}^{2}).

Collecting (
8.39
) and (
8.40
) together, we find that (
8.36
) holds true.

Again thanks to Proposition
8.4
and (
4.24
), we obtain that

\displaystyle|\langle R_{0},g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|R_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha\right)\right)\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}(1+z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}})^{-1}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}(1-\eta)\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}).

Along similar way as (
8.40
), we see that

\displaystyle|\langle R_{1},g\rangle_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}|\lesssim_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-2}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{4}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}^{2}).

The combination of two estimates above gives that (
8.37
) holds.

We then use the similar derivation, to get that

\displaystyle|\langle R_{0},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|R_{0}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}

(8.41)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\left(\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha\right)\right)\|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}(1+z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}})^{-1}\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}((1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}_{\eta}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2})\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}),

\displaystyle|\langle R_{1},g\rangle_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}(\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha))\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

(8.42)

According to (
8.41
) and (
8.42
), we obtain (
8.38
) immediately.
∎

Employing Propositions
5.4
,
7.6
,
7.9
,
8.3
and
8.5
, we get the following proposition directly.
For simplicity, we omit its proof here.

Proposition 8.6
.

Let
L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(0)=0
. There exist constants
\alpha>0
sufficiently small and
\eta(\beta)
, such that if
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
and
|\mu|\leq\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}
, then

\displaystyle\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-3}(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{4}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})

\displaystyle~~~+C_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}},

\displaystyle\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-3}(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{4}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})

\displaystyle~~~+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+C_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha^{-1}(\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}

\displaystyle~~~+C_{\beta}\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+C_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

8.2.  Constructing the solutions

With Proposition
8.4
and Proposition
8.6
at hand, we begin to prove Theorem
1.5
in this subsection.

Proof of Theorem
1.5
.

We first introduce a “fake” time variable
\tau
, and then consider the evolution equation for the function
g(\tau,z,\theta)
as follows:

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}g+\mathcal{L}_{\Gamma}(g)=-T+R_{0}+R_{1}+R_{2},~~~g(0,z,\theta)=0,

(8.43)

where
T
and
R_{i}
with
i=0,1,2
as in (
8
).
We observe that
\mu
is now a function of
\tau
.
Referring back to the definition of
\gamma
in (
4.23
), it follows that
\gamma
consequently depends on
\tau
as well.
This dependency implies that
F^{\ast}_{\gamma}
is ultimately parameterized by
\tau
.

By using the compactness method of evolution equation, we obtain a local solution
g(\tau,z,\theta)
for equation (
8.43
).
Recalling (
8.43
) and the definition of
\mu
in
\eqref{8eq1}_{3}
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(\tau,0)+L^{-1}_{z,K}(\mathcal{L}_{\Gamma}(g))(\tau,0)=L^{-1}_{z,K}(-T+R_{0}+R_{1}+R_{2})(0,\tau)=L^{-1}_{z,K}(R_{1})(\tau,0).

This implies that

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(\tau,0)=-(1+\mu)L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(\tau,0).

Since
L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(0,0)=0
, we infer

L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)(\tau,0)=0
for all
\tau\geq 0
.
We recall the equation (
8.43
) and apply Proposition
8.6
, to discover that

\displaystyle~~~\frac{d}{d\tau}(\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}})+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}

(8.44)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-5}(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2})+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}},

and

\displaystyle~~~\frac{d}{d\tau}(\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}})+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}

(8.45)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}(1-\eta)^{-5}(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{3}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{-1}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{2})+\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-\frac{9}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}

\displaystyle+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\alpha^{-1}(\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}})\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}(1-\eta)^{-2}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}^{2}}.

It then follows from Proposition
8.4
that

\displaystyle|\mu|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}\alpha^{-1}(1-\eta)\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}_{\eta}}+(1-\eta)^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\alpha^{-2}\|g\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}+\alpha).

(8.46)

Since
g|_{\tau=0}=0
, we deduce from (
8.44
)-(
8.46
) and the continuity argument that for all
\tau\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha^{2},~~~\|g\|_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}},~~~|\mu|\lesssim_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha.

(8.47)

Thus, we can obtain that there exists a global solution
g(\tau,z,\theta)
for equation (
8.43
), which satisfies (
8.47
).

We apply the operator
\partial_{\tau}
to equation
8.43
and consider the following evolution equation for the function
\partial_{\tau}g(\tau,z,\theta)
:

\displaystyle\partial^{2}_{\tau}g+\mathcal{L}_{\Gamma}(\partial_{\tau}g)=-\partial_{\tau}T+\partial_{\tau}R_{0}+\partial_{\tau}R_{1}+\partial_{\tau}R_{2}+\bar{R},~~~\partial_{\tau}g(0,z,\theta)=\alpha^{2},

(8.48)

where
\bar{R}\triangleq\mathcal{L}_{\Gamma}(\partial_{\tau}g)-\partial_{\tau}\mathcal{L}_{\Gamma}(g)
.
Note that
(g,F^{\ast}_{\gamma},\gamma,\mu)
are all dependent of
\tau
.
Firstly, it follows from some direct calculations that

\displaystyle|\partial_{\tau}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}(1+|\ln z|+|\ln K(\theta)|)|\partial_{\tau}\gamma|,

(8.49)

\displaystyle|\partial_{\tau}\gamma|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta}|\partial_{\tau}\mu|.

(8.50)

We recall the definition of
\mu
in
\eqref{8eq1}_{3}
, and employ the absorption method and Corollary
7.2
, to obtain that

\displaystyle|\partial_{\tau}\mu|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha^{-1}\|\partial_{\tau}g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}.

(8.51)

Then, we define the
\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{1}([0,\infty)\times[0,\frac{\pi}{2}])
norm
and

\mathcal{E}^{1}_{\eta}([0,\infty)\times[0,\frac{\pi}{2}])
norm as follows:

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{1}}

\displaystyle\triangleq(1-\eta)^{2}(\|fw^{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|D_{z}fw^{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}})+(1-\eta)^{-2}\|D_{\theta}fw^{\lambda}\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

\displaystyle\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{E}^{1}_{\eta}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\alpha(1-\eta)^{2}(\|fw^{\lambda}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|D_{z}fw^{\lambda}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}).

Similar argument as the proof of (
8.45
), we infer from (
8.47
)-(
8.51
) and
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}(\|\partial_{\tau}g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|\partial_{\tau}g\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{1}}+\|\partial_{\tau}g\|_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{1}})+\|\partial_{\tau}g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|\partial_{\tau}g\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{1}}+\|\partial_{\tau}g\|_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{1}}\leq 0.

(8.52)

Owing to
\partial_{\tau}g|_{\tau=0}=\alpha^{2}
, we deduce from (
8.49
)-(
8.52
) and the continuity argument that for all
\tau\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\tau}g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{-1}}+\|\partial_{\tau}g\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\eta}^{1}}+\|\partial_{\tau}g\|_{\mathcal{E}_{\eta}^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha^{2}e^{-\frac{\tau}{100}},

\displaystyle|\partial_{\tau}F^{\ast}_{\gamma}|+|\partial_{\tau}\gamma|+|\partial_{\tau}\mu|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha e^{-\frac{\tau}{100}}.

Taking
\tau\rightarrow\infty
in (
8.43
) and applying the above two inequalities, we obtain that there exists a solution
g(z,\theta)
for equation (
8
).
This completes the proof of Theorem
1.5
.
∎

8.3.  Blow-up phenomenon

Based on Theorem
1.5
, our goal in this subsection is to prove Theorem
1.1
.

Proof of Theorem
1.1
.

We divide this proof into three steps.

Step 1: Proof of estimates
(
1.13
) and (
1.14
).

By virtue of (
3.18
) and (
8.47
), we find that

\displaystyle\|\omega(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}\left|F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\left(1,\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\right|-\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}\big\|g\big\|_{L^{\infty}}\geq\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}\frac{\alpha}{3\gamma}-\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}\big\|g\big\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\geq\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}\frac{\alpha}{3\gamma}-\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}~C_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha^{2}\geq\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}\frac{\alpha}{6\gamma},

where we have used the fact that
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
.
This implies that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\|\omega(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}ds\geq\frac{\alpha}{6\gamma}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{s_{\gamma}}ds=\frac{\alpha}{6\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\beta}{2\gamma}\right)\left|\ln\left(1-\frac{2\gamma}{\gamma+\beta}{t}\right)\right|.

Taking
t\rightarrow T^{\ast}\triangleq\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\beta}{2\gamma}
, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow T^{\ast}}\int_{0}^{t}\|\omega(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}ds=+\infty.

After some direct calculations, one obtains that

\displaystyle\frac{u_{r}}{r}(t)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}(\Psi)=\mathcal{R}\left(\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}\Phi(z,\theta)\right)

(8.53)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}\left(\mathcal{R}(\Phi-G^{\ast}_{F}\sin(2\theta))+\frac{3}{2\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})+\frac{3}{2\alpha}L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)-\frac{3}{2}(\sin\theta)^{2}\langle F,K\rangle_{\theta}\right).

The main term in the right-hand side of the equality above is

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2\alpha}\Big\|L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}=\|L^{-1}_{z}(\Gamma^{\ast}_{\gamma})\|_{L^{\infty}}=\|\frac{2}{1+z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}}\|_{L^{\infty}}=2.

Substituting the equality above into (
8.53
) and applying the fact that
\alpha\ll 1-\eta\ll\beta
, we get that

\displaystyle\Big\|\frac{u_{r}}{r}(t)\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2}{t_{\gamma}}-\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}\left(\|\mathcal{R}(\Phi-G^{\ast}_{F}\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}}+\frac{3}{2\alpha}\|L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\frac{3}{2}\|(\sin\theta)^{2}\langle F,K\rangle_{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2}{t_{\gamma}}-\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}C_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}\geq\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}},

which gives that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big\|\frac{u_{r}}{r}(s)\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}ds\geq\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{s_{\gamma}}ds=\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\beta}{2\gamma}\right)\left|\ln\left(1-\frac{2\gamma}{\gamma+\beta}{t}\right)\right|.

Taking
t\rightarrow T^{\ast}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow T^{\ast}}\int_{0}^{t}\Big\|\frac{u_{r}}{r}(s)\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}ds=+\infty.

It remains to verify (
1.14
). By using (
8.53
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\||\bm{x}|^{\frac{\alpha}{2}-1}u_{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq

\displaystyle\|R^{\frac{1}{2}}r^{-1}u_{r}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq t_{\gamma}^{-1+\beta}\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}(\Phi)\|_{L^{\infty}}

(8.54)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle t_{\gamma}^{-1+\beta}\Big(\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}-G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{g}-G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}\alpha^{-1}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}(\sin\theta)^{2}\langle F^{\ast}_{\gamma},K\rangle_{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\alpha^{-1}L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)-(\sin\theta)^{2}\langle g,K\rangle_{\theta}\big)\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big).

We now analyze each term on the right-hand side of (
8.54
) sequentially.
It is easy to observe that

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in(0,\frac{\pi}{2})}\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{g}-G^{\ast}_{g}\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}[1,\infty)}\lesssim\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{1-\eta}\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}};

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in(0,\frac{\pi}{2})}\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}\alpha^{-1}L^{-1}_{z,K}(F^{\ast}_{\gamma})\|_{L^{\infty}}=2\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}})^{-1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq 2;

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in(0,\frac{\pi}{2})}\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}(\sin\theta)^{2}\langle F^{\ast}_{\gamma},K\rangle_{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\alpha\|z^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{\gamma}}(1+z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}})^{-2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\alpha;

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in(0,\frac{\pi}{2})}\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}\big(\alpha^{-1}L^{-1}_{z,K}(g)-(\sin\theta)^{2}\langle g,K\rangle_{\theta}\big)\|_{L^{\infty}[1,\infty)}\lesssim\alpha^{-1}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{1-\eta}\alpha.

The first term on the right-hand side of (
8.54
) still requires analysis.
To control this term, we need to choose another weight as
z
goes to infinity.
Let us define

\displaystyle w_{z}^{\ast\ast}(z)\triangleq\frac{1+z^{\frac{1}{\beta}+\frac{1}{4}}}{z^{\frac{1}{\beta}+\frac{1}{4}}},

which tends to
1
as
z
goes to infinity.
By some direct calculations, one can check that

\displaystyle\|\Gamma_{\gamma}^{\ast}w_{z}^{\ast\ast}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{z^{\frac{2}{\gamma}}}{(1+z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}})^{4}}\frac{(1+z^{\frac{1}{\beta}+\frac{1}{4}})^{2}}{z^{\frac{2}{\beta}+\frac{1}{2}}}dz\lesssim\int_{0}^{\infty}z^{-\frac{3}{4}}(1+z)^{-1}dz\lesssim 1.

Then, we define the space
H^{k,\ast\ast}
by replacing the radial weight
w_{z}^{\ast}
in the definition of
H^{k,\ast}
with
w_{z}^{\ast\ast}
.
Along with a similar way, this yields that

\displaystyle\|z^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}(\Phi_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}-G^{\ast}_{F^{\ast}_{\gamma}}\sin(2\theta))\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|F_{\gamma}^{\ast}\|_{H^{k,\ast\ast}}\lesssim_{1-\eta}\alpha^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Collecting all estimates above, it follows from (
8.54
) that for any
p\in[1,\frac{2}{2-\beta})
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T^{\ast}}\||\bm{x}|^{\frac{\alpha}{2}-1}u_{r}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{p}ds\lesssim\int_{0}^{T^{\ast}}s_{\gamma}^{(-1+\frac{\beta}{2})p}ds\lesssim 1.

(8.55)

This implies that
u_{r}\in L^{p}([0,T^{\ast}];L^{\infty}_{loc})
.
As for
u_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{u_{3}}{|\bm{x}|}=-\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}\big((\cos\theta)^{-1}\Phi+2\cos\theta\Phi+\alpha\cos\theta D_{z}\Phi-\sin\theta\partial_{\theta}\Phi\big).

By adapting the derivation method employed in (
8.55
), we derive that
u_{3}\in L^{p}([0,T^{\ast}];L^{\infty}_{loc})
.

Step 2: The Hölder regularity.

First, we intend to show the Hölder regularity of the initial data.
According to (
8.47
), we have

\displaystyle\|gw_{z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}L^{2}_{z}}+\|D_{z}gw_{z}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\theta}L^{2}_{z}}\lesssim\alpha^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|g\|_{\mathcal{H}^{2}}\lesssim_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}.

This, together with the definition of
w_{z}
, yields directly that

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in(0,\frac{\pi}{2})}\left(\|g\|_{L^{2}_{z}[1,\infty)}+\|D_{z}g\|_{L^{2}_{z}[1,\infty)}+\|gz^{-\frac{2}{\beta}}\|_{L^{2}_{z}[0,1]}+\|D_{z}gz^{-\frac{2}{\beta}}\|_{L^{2}_{z}[0,1]}\right)\lesssim_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}.

(8.56)

We claim that

\displaystyle g=O(z^{-\frac{1}{2}}),\quad\text{as}~~z\rightarrow\infty;\quad g=O(z^{\frac{2}{\beta}-\frac{1}{2}}),\quad\text{as}~~z\rightarrow 0.

(8.57)

Indeed, it follows from (
8.56
) that for any
z\in[1,\infty)
and
\theta\in(0,\frac{\pi}{2})
,

\displaystyle|g(z,\theta)|^{2}=\left|\int_{z}^{\infty}g\partial_{z^{\prime}}gdz^{\prime}\right|\lesssim z^{-1}\sup_{\theta\in(0,\frac{\pi}{2})}\left(\|g\|_{L^{2}_{z}[1,\infty)}\|D_{z}g\|_{L^{2}_{z}[1,\infty)}\right)\lesssim_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha^{3}z^{-1}.

Another estimate in (
8.57
) can be deduced in a similar way, and we thus omit its proof here.
Recalling (
3.18
), one gets, by some direct calculations, that

\displaystyle F^{\ast}_{\gamma}=O(z^{-\frac{1}{\gamma}}),\quad\text{as}~~z\rightarrow\infty;\quad F^{\ast}_{\gamma}=O(z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}),\quad\text{as}~~z\rightarrow 0.

(8.58)

Since
F=F^{\ast}_{\gamma}+g
, we can find that

\displaystyle F=O(z^{-\frac{1}{2}}),\quad\text{as}~~z\rightarrow\infty;\quad F=O(z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}),\quad\text{as}~~z\rightarrow 0.

(8.59)

It is easy to check that

\displaystyle\omega_{0}(r,x_{3})=\Omega(0,R,\theta)=F(R,\theta).

This, together with
z|_{t=0}=R=|x|^{\alpha}
and (
8.59
), gives that

\displaystyle|\omega_{0}(r,x_{3})|\lesssim\frac{|x|^{\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}}}{(1+|x|)^{\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}+\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\lesssim\frac{|x|^{\frac{\alpha}{2\beta}}}{(1+|x|)^{\frac{\alpha}{2\beta}+\frac{\alpha}{2}}},

where we have used the fact that
\gamma=\frac{1+\mu}{1-\mu}\beta
and
\mu
is small enough.

Next, we want to prove the Hölder regularity for the solution.
From (
3.18
), it follows that for
t=0
,

\displaystyle F^{\ast}_{\gamma}=O((\sin\theta)^{\frac{\alpha}{3\gamma}}(\cos\theta)^{\frac{2\alpha}{3\gamma}}),\quad\text{as}~~\sin(2\theta)\rightarrow 0;\quad F^{\ast}_{\gamma}=O(z^{\frac{1}{\gamma}})=O(|x|^{\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}}),\quad\text{as}~~|x|\rightarrow 0.

(8.60)

In view of (
8.47
), we achieve that

\displaystyle\sup_{z\in[0,\infty)}\left(\|(\sin(2\theta))^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}g\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}+\|(\sin(2\theta))^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}D_{\theta}g\|_{L^{2}_{\theta}}\right)\lesssim_{\beta,1-\eta}\alpha^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Along a similar way as (
8.57
), we find that for
t=0
,

\displaystyle g=O((\sin(2\theta))^{\frac{\lambda-1}{2}})=O((\sin(2\theta))^{\frac{\alpha}{20\beta}}),\quad\text{as}~~\sin(2\theta)\rightarrow 0;\quad g=O(|x|^{\frac{2\alpha}{\beta}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}),\quad\text{as}~~|x|\rightarrow 0.

(8.61)

Owing to
r=|x|\cos(\theta)
and
x_{3}=|x|\sin(\theta)
, we get, by applying (
8.60
) and (
8.61
), that

\displaystyle F^{\ast}_{\gamma}=O(r^{\frac{2\alpha}{3\gamma}}|x_{3}|^{\frac{\alpha}{3\gamma}}),\quad g=O((r|x_{3}|)^{\frac{\alpha}{20\beta}}|x|^{\frac{19\alpha}{10\gamma}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}),\quad\text{as}~~r\rightarrow 0~~\text{or}~~x_{3}\rightarrow 0.

This yields directly that for
t=0
,

F^{\ast}_{\gamma}\in C_{x}^{\frac{\alpha}{3\gamma}}(D_{0})
and
g\in C_{x}^{\frac{\alpha}{20\beta}}(D_{0})
.
We thus deduce that
\omega_{0}\in C_{x}^{\frac{\alpha}{20\beta}}(D_{0})
.

For any
t\in[0,T^{\ast})
, we have

\displaystyle|\omega(t,r,x_{3})-\omega_{0}(r,x_{3})|=

\displaystyle|\Omega(t,R,\theta)-\Omega(0,R,\theta)|\leq\left|\left(\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}-1\right)F(z,\theta)\right|+|F(z,\theta)-F(R,\theta)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle t\|F\|_{L^{\infty}}+|z-R|^{\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}}\leq t^{\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}}.

This yields that
\omega\in C_{t}^{\frac{\alpha}{\gamma}}[0,T^{\ast})
. Therefore, one gets that
\omega\in C_{t}^{\frac{\alpha}{2\beta}}[0,T^{\ast})
.

Step 3: The scaling index.

Finally, we aim to show that the scaling index is
\frac{\beta}{\alpha}
. We can deduce from some computations that

\displaystyle\omega(t,r,x_{3})

\displaystyle=\Omega(t,R,\theta)=\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}F\Big(\frac{R}{t_{\gamma}^{\beta}},\theta\Big)=\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}F\Big(\Big(\frac{|x|}{t_{\gamma}^{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}}\Big)^{\alpha},\arctan\Big(\frac{x_{3}}{r}\Big)\Big)

(8.62)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}F\Big(\Big(\Big(\frac{r}{t_{\gamma}^{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}}\Big)^{2}+\Big(\frac{x_{3}}{t_{\gamma}^{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}}\Big)^{2}\Big)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}},\arctan\Big(\frac{x_{3}}{t_{\gamma}^{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}}\Big(\frac{r}{t_{\gamma}^{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}}\Big)^{-1}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{t_{\gamma}}H\Big(\frac{r}{t_{\gamma}^{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}},\frac{x_{3}}{t_{\gamma}^{\frac{\beta}{\alpha}}}\Big),

where
H
is defined by

\displaystyle H(x,y)\triangleq F\big((x^{2}+y^{2})^{\frac{\alpha}{2}},\arctan(yx^{-1})\big).

The equality (
8.62
) implies that the scaling index is
\frac{\beta}{\alpha}
.
We thereby finish the proof of Theorem
1.1
.
∎
